# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - FALLOUT LIVING-WORLD EVENT EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# Dormant, idempotent scheduler initialization for the reveal timeline, stable
# successor registry, country runtime rows, orientation receipts, cooldown
# slots, fatigue, deterministic candidate scoring, a hard family-repeat veto,
# and generation-bound ordinary-opening, arc, delayed-result, bilateral,
# cancellation, cleanup, and exact dispatch-issuance receipts. The numerical
# review lanes remain dormant behind the existing activation flags. No gameplay
# caller selects a candidate or reserves an event.
# ============================================================================

# Engine contract for public transaction APIs: the outer caller must create
# every documented temporary output before invoking the scripted effect. The
# effect overwrites those existing temporary variables with its final receipt.

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register.
fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register > constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register < constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.upper_bound }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
		}
		set_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_scheduler_error_code = fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_event_scheduler_error_count } }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_scheduler_error_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.fallout_event_scheduler_error_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Idempotent day-based fatigue decay. Family values
# are scheduler-owned and never read from the live survival ledger.
fallout_event_decay_family_fatigue = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = fallout_event_runtime_initialized
			has_variable = fallout_event_fatigue_last_update_day
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_elapsed_days = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_elapsed_days = fallout_event_fatigue_last_update_day }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_fatigue_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_decay_amount = fallout_event_fatigue_elapsed_days }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_decay_amount = constant:fallout_event_runtime.fatigue_decay_per_day }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index
				end = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.upper_bound
				value = fallout_event_fatigue_index
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_fatigue_index > constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.none }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_value = fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries^fallout_event_fatigue_index }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_value = fallout_event_fatigue_decay_amount }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_fatigue_value < constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries^fallout_event_fatigue_index = fallout_event_fatigue_value }
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_last_update_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Applies the one-time +60 opening increment and
# records durable completion/opening provenance after the issued receipt is
# written. Hidden AI openings update completion but not visible popup history.
fallout_event_apply_opening_fatigue_and_history = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.ordinary_event }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket value = fallout_event_ordinary_ticket compare = equals }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_fatigue_applied_ticket value = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket compare = equals } }
		}
		fallout_event_decay_family_fatigue = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_opening_fatigue_value = fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries^fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_family }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_opening_fatigue_value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.fatigue_opening_add }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_opening_fatigue_value > constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_fatigue }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_opening_fatigue_value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_fatigue }
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries^fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_family = fallout_event_opening_fatigue_value }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cooldown_family = fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_family }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_applied_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_last_visible_popup_day = global.num_days }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.state } }
				remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_type_entries index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
				remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_entries index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
				remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_day_entries index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
				remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_event_entries index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_type_entries value = fallout_event_ordinary_target_type }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_entries value = fallout_event_ordinary_target }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_day_entries value = global.num_days }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_event_entries value = fallout_event_ordinary_human_event_token }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_completion_match_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_completion_match_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		for_each_loop = {
			array = fallout_event_candidate_completion_id_entries
			value = fallout_event_completion_stored_id
			index = fallout_event_completion_stored_index
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_completion_stored_id value = fallout_event_ordinary_candidate_id compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_completion_match_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_completion_match_index = fallout_event_completion_stored_index }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_completion_match_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_completion_id_entries index = fallout_event_completion_match_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_completion_day_entries index = fallout_event_completion_match_index }
		}
		add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_completion_id_entries value = fallout_event_ordinary_candidate_id }
		add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_completion_day_entries value = global.num_days }
		fallout_event_record_bilateral_family_memory = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Records a relationship opening's exact partner and
# cooldown family on both current runtime rows. The issue receipt is the only
# call site, so retries cannot duplicate the 90-day pair memory.
fallout_event_record_bilateral_family_memory = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index < global.fallout_event_registry_count }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_memory_owner_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_memory_family = fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_family }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_memory_day = global.num_days }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_memory_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
					fallout_event_registry_country_identity_is_current = yes
					fallout_event_registry_country_survival_binding_is_current = yes
					fallout_event_partner_runtime_state_is_current = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_memory_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_memory_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_memory_partner_entries value = fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index }
			add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_memory_family_entries value = fallout_event_memory_family }
			add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_memory_day_entries value = fallout_event_memory_day }
			var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index = {
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_memory_partner_entries value = fallout_event_memory_owner_registry_index }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_memory_family_entries value = fallout_event_memory_family }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_memory_day_entries value = fallout_event_memory_day }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Output: fallout_event_base_cooldown_days. The
# phase/size table is the sole source for ordinary cadence and broadcast
# extensions. Ash week deliberately returns zero.
fallout_event_calculate_base_cooldown_days = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_season } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.first_season.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.first_season.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.first_season.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_winter_year } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.first_winter_year.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.first_winter_year.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.first_winter_year.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.consolidation } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.consolidation.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.consolidation.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.consolidation.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.rival_orders } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.rival_orders.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.rival_orders.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.rival_orders.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.new_states } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.new_states.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.new_states.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.new_states.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.soot_retreat } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.soot_retreat.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.soot_retreat.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.soot_retreat.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.second_world } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.second_world.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.second_world.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.second_world.large } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.open_continuation } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.open_continuation.small } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.open_continuation.medium } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_days.open_continuation.large } }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. The committed-player target array is
# the sole bounded recipient list. Each current recipient gets an extension,
# and the max-reservation helper never shortens an existing due day.
fallout_event_extend_broadcast_player_cooldowns = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = fallout_player_continuation_commit_complete }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.fallout_player_committed_targets
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			fallout_event_calculate_base_cooldown_days = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
					check_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days = fallout_event_base_cooldown_days }
				fallout_event_apply_requested_ordinary_cooldown = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_candidate_validation_index.
# Normalizes the accepted resource and Air Winter identities before scoring.
fallout_event_normalize_candidate_pressure = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_index_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index > constant:fallout_survival_resource.none }
			fallout_survival_country_row_is_current = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_index = fallout_event_candidate_resource_index_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.resource_pressure_midpoint }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = fallout_survival_current_entries^fallout_event_candidate_resource_index }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure < constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.resource_pressure_multiplier }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_one } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase_pressure.phase_one } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_two } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase_pressure.phase_two } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_three } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase_pressure.phase_three } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_four } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase_pressure.phase_four } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_five } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase_pressure.phase_five } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_six } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase_pressure.phase_six } }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_candidate_validation_index.
# The score is rounded exactly once at the end and is never used to bypass a
# row's hard eligibility or receipt provenance proof.
fallout_event_calculate_candidate_score = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_relevance_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_state_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	fallout_event_normalize_candidate_pressure = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.global_survival_and_society } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.global_survival_and_society } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.regional_and_biome } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.regional_and_biome } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.government_archetype } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.government_archetype } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.successor_country_memory } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.successor_country_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.character_and_leader } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.character_and_leader } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.diplomacy_trade_war_settlement } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.diplomacy_trade_war_settlement } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.cause_memory_and_altered_fiction } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.cause_memory_and_altered_fiction } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.recovery_and_late_world_order } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_primary_family_weight.recovery_and_late_world_order } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = global.fallout_event_current_phase }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_phase_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.preferred }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_phase_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.required }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.preferred_phase }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_secondary_phase_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = global.fallout_event_current_phase }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_phase_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.preferred }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_phase_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.secondary }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.supported_secondary_phase }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_region_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_region } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_government_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_government_archetype } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_country_memory_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_country_memory } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_cause_memory_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_terminal_cause_memory } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_winter_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_air_winter_condition } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_crisis_resource_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_survival_resource_crisis } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_character_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.recurring_character_available } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_bilateral_opportunity_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.bilateral_opportunity } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_route_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.exact_route } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_previous_choice_match_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.previous_choice } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_recent_war_or_crisis_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.recent_war_or_crisis } }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_severity_score = fallout_event_candidate_severity_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_severity_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.severity_factor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_severity_score }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_pressure_source_type_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = fallout_event_candidate_mechanic_pressure_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_pressure_source_type_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.survival_resource } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_pressure_source_type_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.air_winter } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure } }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.mechanic_pressure_factor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_relevance_score = fallout_event_candidate_player_relevance_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_relevance_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.player_relevance_factor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_relevance_score }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_state_score = fallout_event_candidate_state_value_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_state_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.state_value_factor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_state_score }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_authored_adjustment_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_fatigue_index = fallout_event_candidate_cooldown_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_fatigue_score = fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries^fallout_event_candidate_fatigue_index }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_fatigue_score }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_candidate_has_recent_state_match = yes }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.repeated_state_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_repeatable_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_repeatability.repeatable }
			fallout_event_candidate_has_recent_completion = yes
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.repeatable_event_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_parent_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.arc_capacity_zero_bonus } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count > constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } } subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_score_contribution.arc_capacity_two_penalty } }
	}
	round_temp_variable = fallout_event_candidate_score
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_candidate_validation_index.
fallout_event_set_candidate_tie_priority = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.broadcast }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_class.crisis_incident } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.crisis_incident } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.major_arc } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.relationship } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_class.routine_incident } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.routine_incident } }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. The selected row is reserved only after every
# current receipt and cadence gate is revalidated. Selection-day state is
# committed before any future dispatch can retry the same date.
fallout_event_commit_selected_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selection_commit_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = fallout_event_selected_candidate_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			fallout_event_candidate_row_is_eligible = yes
			fallout_event_numerical_selection_slot_is_open = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		fallout_event_calculate_candidate_score = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_score value = fallout_event_selected_candidate_score compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			fallout_event_calculate_base_cooldown_days = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_due_day = global.num_days }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_crisis_break_used = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_crisis_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.eligible }
					check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_due_day compare = less_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_due_delay = fallout_event_candidate_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_due_delay = constant:fallout_event_runtime.crisis_break_days_per_budget }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_crisis_break_used = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_due_delay = fallout_event_base_cooldown_days }
				multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_due_delay = fallout_event_candidate_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_selected_due_delay > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_transaction_key = fallout_event_candidate_transaction_key_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_human_event_token = fallout_event_candidate_human_event_token_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token = fallout_event_candidate_ai_event_token_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_branch = fallout_event_candidate_branch_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_due_day = global.num_days }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_issue_day = global.num_days }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_primary_family = fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family = fallout_event_candidate_cooldown_family_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_type = fallout_event_candidate_target_type_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target = fallout_event_candidate_target_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_phase = global.fallout_event_current_phase }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_mode = fallout_event_candidate_crisis_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_break_used = fallout_event_selected_crisis_break_used }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_candidate_id = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_score = fallout_event_candidate_score }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost = fallout_event_candidate_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index = fallout_event_candidate_partner_registry_index_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket = fallout_event_candidate_parent_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_actor_type = fallout_event_candidate_actor_type_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_actor = fallout_event_candidate_actor_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_repeatable = fallout_event_candidate_repeatable_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_route = fallout_event_candidate_route_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = fallout_event_review_control_mode }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship } }
					fallout_event_schedule_selected_relationship_candidate = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc } }
					fallout_event_schedule_selected_major_arc_candidate = yes
				}
				else = {
					fallout_event_schedule_ordinary_receipt = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selection_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selection_day = global.num_days }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_score = fallout_event_candidate_score }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_candidate_id = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_class = fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_crisis_mode = fallout_event_candidate_crisis_mode_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_target_type = fallout_event_candidate_target_type_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_target = fallout_event_candidate_target_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_partner_registry_index = fallout_event_candidate_partner_registry_index_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_break_used = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_variable = fallout_event_last_crisis_break_day }
								NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_last_crisis_break_day value = fallout_event_requested_issue_day compare = equals } }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { fallout_event_last_crisis_break_day = fallout_event_requested_issue_day }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship } } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days = fallout_event_selected_due_delay }
						fallout_event_apply_requested_ordinary_cooldown = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = constant:fallout_event_class.broadcast }
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_generation }
								NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_day }
								NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_ticket }
								NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_generation value = fallout_event_runtime_generation compare = equals } }
								NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_day value = global.num_days compare = equals } }
								NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_ticket value = fallout_event_transaction_ticket compare = equals } }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { global.fallout_event_last_broadcast_day = global.num_days }
						set_variable = { global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
						set_variable = { global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_day = global.num_days }
						set_variable = { global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
						set_global_flag = fallout_event_global_broadcast_receipt_initialized
						fallout_event_extend_broadcast_player_cooldowns = yes
					}
					# Ordinary openings are issued on the commit day. Major and
					# relationship reservations wait for their authored stage or
					# relationship consumers before any dispatch is issued.
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship }
									check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc }
								}
							}
							NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
						}
						fallout_event_reconcile_ordinary_receipt = yes
						if = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issue_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
							fallout_event_issue_dispatch_envelope = yes
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selection_commit_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Full deterministic scan of the reviewed candidate
# arrays. No random trigger or random_list is used. Equal scores resolve by
# class priority, candidate id, target id, and partner registry index.
fallout_event_select_reviewed_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.broadcast }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_target = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_partner = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_numerical_selection_slot_is_open = yes }
		fallout_event_decay_family_fatigue = yes
		for_each_loop = {
			array = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries
			value = fallout_event_candidate_loop_id
			index = fallout_event_candidate_loop_index
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_validation_index = fallout_event_candidate_loop_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_candidate_row_is_eligible = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_resource_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_air_winter_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_unresolved_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority = constant:fallout_event_tie_priority.broadcast }
				fallout_event_calculate_candidate_score = yes
				fallout_event_set_candidate_tie_priority = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_score > fallout_event_selected_candidate_score }
							AND = {
								check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_score value = fallout_event_selected_candidate_score compare = equals }
								OR = {
									check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority < fallout_event_selected_candidate_priority }
									AND = {
										check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority value = fallout_event_selected_candidate_priority compare = equals }
										OR = {
											check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_loop_id < fallout_event_selected_candidate_id }
											AND = {
												check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_loop_id value = fallout_event_selected_candidate_id compare = equals }
												OR = {
													check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_target_entries^fallout_event_candidate_loop_index < fallout_event_selected_candidate_target }
													AND = {
														check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_target_entries^fallout_event_candidate_loop_index value = fallout_event_selected_candidate_target compare = equals }
														check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_partner_registry_index_entries^fallout_event_candidate_loop_index < fallout_event_selected_candidate_partner }
													}
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_index = fallout_event_candidate_loop_index }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_score = fallout_event_candidate_score }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_priority = fallout_event_candidate_tie_priority }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_id = fallout_event_candidate_loop_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_target = fallout_event_candidate_target_entries^fallout_event_candidate_loop_index }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_selected_candidate_partner = fallout_event_candidate_partner_registry_index_entries^fallout_event_candidate_loop_index }
				}
			}
		}
		fallout_event_commit_selected_candidate = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. The human lane is sourced only from
# the frozen continuation commit target array and receipt flag.
fallout_event_review_human_successors = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_human_review_lane_is_approved = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.fallout_player_committed_targets
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
					}
				fallout_event_reconcile_country_transactions = yes
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_candidate_registry_is_reviewed = yes }
					fallout_event_select_reviewed_candidate = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. The AI lane is a bounded stable scan
# over the frozen registry, with one-to-four rows selected by registry-count band.
fallout_event_review_ai_registry_batch = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_ai_review_lane_is_approved = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_registry_count = global.fallout_event_registry_count }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_batch_size = constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.maximum_batch }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_review_registry_count < constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.first_threshold }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_batch_size = constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.first_batch }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_review_registry_count < constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.second_threshold }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_batch_size = constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.second_batch }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_review_registry_count < constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.third_threshold }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_batch_size = constant:fallout_event_ai_review_batch.third_batch }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_processed_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_break = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_index = global.fallout_event_review_cursor }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index
			end = global.fallout_event_registry_count
			value = fallout_event_review_scan_offset
			break = fallout_event_review_scan_break
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_offset = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_index = global.fallout_event_review_cursor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_review_scan_index value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_index = global.fallout_event_registry_count }
			}
			var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_review_scan_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
						fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
					}
					fallout_event_reconcile_country_transactions = yes
					if = {
						limit = { fallout_event_candidate_registry_is_reviewed = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
						fallout_event_select_reviewed_candidate = yes
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_processed_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_review_processed_count value = fallout_event_review_batch_size compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_break = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_review_scan_index value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_review_scan_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_review_cursor = fallout_event_review_scan_index }
		}
	}
}

}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Converts only a provably empty schema-v1 receipt
# to the current empty schema. Pending v1 payloads are never rewritten.
fallout_event_migrate_empty_ordinary_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_empty_ordinary_receipt_v1_can_migrate = yes }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_schema.version }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. This backward-compatible promotion writes only an
# empty fixed-receipt schema while ordinary production remains disabled.
fallout_event_promote_empty_ordinary_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_empty_ordinary_receipt_can_promote = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_empty_ordinary_receipt_v1_can_migrate = yes }
			fallout_event_migrate_empty_ordinary_receipt = yes
		}
		else = {
			fallout_event_initialize_empty_ordinary_receipt = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. This bounded one-time pass touches only
# the frozen registry and never constructs a game-wide country pool.
fallout_event_promote_registry_empty_ordinary_receipts = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_ready }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_activation_approved }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			has_variable = global.fallout_transition_generation
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_generation
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_count
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.version }
				check_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.previous_without_survival_binding }
			}
			check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_generation value = global.fallout_transition_generation compare = equals }
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_count > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_countries^num compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation_entries^num compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_index_entries^num compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_proven_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			array = global.fallout_event_registry_index_entries
			value = fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_stored_index
			index = fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_index
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_stored_index value = fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_generation_entries^fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_index value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation compare = equals }
				}
				var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_index = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_runtime_initialized
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_index compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_generation value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_runtime_generation value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation compare = equals }
						}
						fallout_event_promote_empty_ordinary_receipt = yes
						if = {
							limit = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_proven_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_promotion_proven_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_generation = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_day = global.num_days }
			set_global_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_ready
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. Re-promotes only empty v1 receipts
# behind an already-published dormant registry header. Pending/nonempty rows
# remain untouched and prevent the proof from being claimed.
fallout_event_repromote_old_ready_empty_ordinary_receipts = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_old_ready_empty_ordinary_receipts_can_repromote = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			array = global.fallout_event_registry_index_entries
			value = fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index_value
			index = fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index_value value = fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_generation_entries^fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation compare = equals }
				}
				var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_runtime_initialized
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_index compare = equals }
						}
						if = {
							limit = { fallout_event_empty_ordinary_receipt_v1_can_migrate = yes }
							fallout_event_migrate_empty_ordinary_receipt = yes
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
								NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending }
							}
							add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_old_ready_ordinary_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_generation = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_day = global.num_days }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. This dormant numerical promotion copies the
# immutable survival state count only while allocation and the survival ledger
# are current. Existing rows must prove complete transaction dormancy.
fallout_event_promote_empty_numerical_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_empty_numerical_receipt_can_promote = yes
			fallout_successor_allocation_is_current = yes
			fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_numerical_schema.version }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_state_count = fallout_survival_country_state_count }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_state_count < constant:fallout_event_runtime.small_state_upper_bound } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_state_count < constant:fallout_event_runtime.medium_state_upper_bound } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large }
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_last_update_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_applied_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_crisis_break_day = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_visible_popup_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selection_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selection_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_class = constant:fallout_event_class.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_crisis_mode = constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_target = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_partner_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cleanup_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cleanup_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_visible_target_type_entries^num = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index
				end = constant:fallout_event_runtime.visible_history_size
				value = fallout_event_numerical_history_initialization_index
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_type_entries value = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_entries value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_day_entries value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
				add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_event_entries value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_initialized
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. This bounded pass promotes only rows
# proven dormant and marks already-current rows as part of the same generation.
fallout_event_promote_registry_numerical_receipts = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_ready }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_activation_approved }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			fallout_event_registry_header_is_current = yes
			fallout_successor_allocation_is_current = yes
			fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_promotion_proven_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			array = global.fallout_event_registry_index_entries
			value = fallout_event_numerical_promotion_stored_index
			index = fallout_event_numerical_promotion_index
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_numerical_promotion_stored_index value = fallout_event_numerical_promotion_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_generation_entries^fallout_event_numerical_promotion_index value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation compare = equals }
				}
				var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_numerical_promotion_index = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_runtime_initialized
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = fallout_event_numerical_promotion_index compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_runtime_generation value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation compare = equals }
						}
						if = {
							limit = { fallout_event_country_runtime_row_is_current = yes }
							set_country_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_initialized
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
						}
						else = {
							fallout_event_promote_empty_numerical_receipt = yes
							if = {
								limit = { fallout_event_country_runtime_row_is_current = yes }
								set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_promotion_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_promotion_proven_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_numerical_promotion_proven_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = equals }
				fallout_event_registry_payload_is_current = yes
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_generation = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_day = global.num_days }
			set_global_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_ready
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.country_row_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs use the fallout_event_requested_dispatch_*
# temporary variables. Output: fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted. The outer
# caller must pre-seed that output. The ready flag is written last as the commit
# marker. This records routing data only and never fires an event.
fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_dispatch_envelope_is_consistent = yes
			exists = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source > constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source < constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode > constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode < constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					}
					fallout_event_subject_shape_is_valid = yes
				}
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_mode = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_branch = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_target_type = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_target = fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Output: fallout_event_dispatch_issue_accepted. The
# outer caller must pre-seed that output. The immutable envelope identity is
# copied first and its issued flag is written last. The dynamic event command
# runs only after that receipt proves current. Every issued source uses an exact
# token-authenticated terminalizer. No detached acknowledgement API exists.
fallout_event_issue_dispatch_envelope = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issue_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_dispatch_issuance_is_approved = yes
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
			fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_absent = yes
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_source = fallout_event_dispatch_source }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_generation = fallout_event_dispatch_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_mode = fallout_event_dispatch_mode }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token = fallout_event_dispatch_event_token }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_branch = fallout_event_dispatch_branch }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_target_type = fallout_event_dispatch_target_type }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_target = fallout_event_dispatch_target }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_date = global.date }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issued_day = global.num_days }
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_issued
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_current = yes
				fallout_event_dispatch_envelope_matches_transaction = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issue_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			fallout_event_apply_opening_fatigue_and_history = yes
			meta_effect = {
				text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.[FALLOUT_EVENT_ID] } }
				FALLOUT_EVENT_ID = "[?fallout_event_dispatch_event_token|.0]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			fallout_event_clear_dispatch_issue_receipt = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.dispatch_issue_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: fallout_event_cancelled_ticket,
# fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record, and cancellation reason.
fallout_event_record_cancellation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record > constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record < constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_cancelled_ticket }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cancellation_source = fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cancellation_reason = fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cancellation_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cancellation_day = global.num_days }
		add_to_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	}
}

# The compact arc arrays define slot order. These flags are derived occupancy
# caches only and never identify an arc.
fallout_event_refresh_arc_slot_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_1_active
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_2_active
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_3_active
	set_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_1_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count > constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_2_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count > constant:fallout_event_runtime.two } }
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_3_active
	}
}

	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_pending
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending

	clear_array = fallout_event_arc_generation_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_arc_primary_family_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_arc_cooldown_family_entries

	clear_array = fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries

	clear_array = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_owner_entries

	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries
