	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_owner_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_parent_arc_ticket_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries
	set_variable = { fallout_event_runtime_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_arc_schema.version }

	set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_schema.version }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	fallout_event_refresh_arc_slot_flags = yes
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. This one-time promotion is valid only because
# schema 1 had no transaction producer and the caller proved every row empty.
fallout_event_migrate_empty_transaction_row = {
	fallout_event_initialize_empty_transaction_ledgers = yes
	set_country_flag = fallout_event_transaction_schema_migrated
	set_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_schema_migration_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_schema_migration_day = global.num_days }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator.
fallout_event_migrate_dormant_transaction_schema = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_dormant_transaction_schema_migration_is_allowed = yes }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries
			fallout_event_migrate_empty_transaction_row = yes
		}
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.version }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_schema = global.fallout_survival_ledger_schema_version }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_generation = global.fallout_survival_ledger_generation }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_country_count = global.fallout_survival_ledger_country_count }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_ticket_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_next_ticket_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_ticket_id }
		fallout_event_promote_registry_empty_ordinary_receipts = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_registry_payload_is_current = yes }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
			clr_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.schema_migration_unproven }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.previous_initialization_only_version }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.schema_migration_unproven }
		fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Upgrades only empty schema-2 compact ledgers. The
# existing ordinary receipt, ticket ledger, and runtime history remain intact.
fallout_event_migrate_current_empty_transaction_row = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_current_runtime_empty_transaction_row_can_migrate = yes }
		clear_array = fallout_event_arc_visible_budget_cost_entries
		clear_array = fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries
		clear_array = fallout_event_delayed_visible_budget_cost_entries
		clear_array = fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries
		clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_visible_budget_cost_entries
		clear_array = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries
		set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_arc_schema.version }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_queue_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_delayed_queue_schema.version }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_schema.version }
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_transaction_schema_migrated
		set_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_schema_migration_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_schema_migration_day = global.num_days }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. This pass is dormant and generation
# bounded. A nonempty old row leaves the migration marker unset and fails the
# global proof rather than fabricating new receipt columns.
fallout_event_migrate_current_empty_transaction_schema = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_current_runtime_empty_transaction_schema_migration_is_allowed = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_current_schema_migration_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries
			fallout_event_migrate_current_empty_transaction_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_transaction_schema_migrated }
				add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_current_schema_migration_count = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_current_schema_migration_count value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = equals } }
			set_global_flag = fallout_event_transaction_schema_v3_migration_complete
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. This promotes only a dormant schema-2
# runtime whose schema-1 registry already matches the committed survival rows.
fallout_event_migrate_registry_survival_binding = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_registry_survival_binding_migration_is_allowed = yes }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.version }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_schema = global.fallout_survival_ledger_schema_version }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_generation = global.fallout_survival_ledger_generation }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_country_count = global.fallout_survival_ledger_country_count }
		fallout_event_promote_registry_empty_ordinary_receipts = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_registry_payload_is_current = yes }
			clr_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.schema_migration_unproven }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
			has_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.previous_without_survival_binding }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.schema_migration_unproven }
		fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Output: fallout_event_transaction_ticket.
fallout_event_allocate_transaction_ticket = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_ticket_ledger_is_current = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = global.fallout_event_next_ticket_id }
		add_to_variable = { global.fallout_event_next_ticket_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY
fallout_event_reset_orientation_receipts = {
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_complete
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_national_complete
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_capital_complete
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_resource_complete
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_archetype_complete

	clear_variable = fallout_event_orientation_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_event_orientation_national_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_event_orientation_capital_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_event_orientation_resource_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_event_orientation_archetype_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_event_orientation_character_generation
	set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_status = constant:fallout_event_orientation_status.pending }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Clears only scheduler-owned transient runtime data.
# Durable future event memories use separate identifiers and are not cleared.
fallout_event_clear_country_runtime = {
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_runtime_initialized
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_initialized
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_candidate_registry_reviewed
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_1_active
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_2_active
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_slot_3_active
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_pending
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_pending
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending
	fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
	fallout_event_clear_ordinary_receipt = yes
	clr_country_flag = fallout_event_transaction_schema_migrated
	fallout_event_reset_orientation_receipts = yes
	clear_array = fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_visible_target_type_entries
	clear_array = fallout_event_visible_target_entries

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Requires the country registry index and generation
# to have been written by the current initialization transaction.
fallout_event_initialize_country_runtime = {
	fallout_event_clear_country_runtime = yes
	set_variable = { fallout_event_runtime_generation = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
	fallout_event_initialize_empty_transaction_ledgers = yes
	fallout_event_reset_orientation_receipts = yes
	set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_orientation_schema.version }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_numerical_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_numerical_schema.version }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cooldown_family = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.none }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_last_update_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_fatigue_applied_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_crisis_break_day = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_visible_popup_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selection_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selection_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_score = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_class = constant:fallout_event_class.none }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_crisis_mode = constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.none }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_target = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_partner_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_selected_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cleanup_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_last_cleanup_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_state_count = fallout_survival_country_state_count }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_state_count < constant:fallout_event_runtime.small_state_upper_bound } }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.small }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_state_count < constant:fallout_event_runtime.medium_state_upper_bound } }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.medium }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { fallout_event_frozen_country_size = constant:fallout_event_country_size.large }
	}
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index
		end = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.upper_bound
		value = fallout_event_fatigue_initialization_index
		add_to_array = {
			array = fallout_event_family_fatigue_entries
			value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero
		}
	}
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index
		end = constant:fallout_event_runtime.visible_history_size
		value = fallout_event_visible_history_initialization_index
		add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_type_entries value = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
		add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_entries value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_target_day_entries value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_visible_event_entries value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_due_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_quiet_period_due_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	add_to_variable = { fallout_event_quiet_period_due_day = constant:fallout_event_runtime.quiet_period_days }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_runtime_initialized_date = global.date }
	set_variable = { fallout_event_runtime_initialized_day = global.num_days }
	set_country_flag = fallout_event_runtime_initialized
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. This is used only before the registry is committed.
fallout_event_reset_uncommitted_registry = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries
		fallout_event_clear_country_runtime = yes
		clr_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
		clear_variable = fallout_event_registry_generation
		clear_variable = fallout_event_registry_index
	}
	clear_array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries
	clear_array = global.fallout_event_registry_generation_entries
	clear_array = global.fallout_event_registry_index_entries
	clr_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
	clr_global_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_ready
	clr_global_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_ready
	clr_global_flag = fallout_event_transaction_schema_v3_migration_complete
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_generation
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_count
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_source_count
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_survival_schema
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_survival_generation
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_survival_country_count
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_cursor
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_initialized_date
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_initialized_day
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_ticket_generation
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_next_ticket_id
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_generation
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_day
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_generation
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_day
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_review_cursor
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_last_broadcast_day
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_generation
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_day
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_ticket
	clr_global_flag = fallout_event_global_broadcast_receipt_initialized
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. The source allocation array is frozen in its exact
# deterministic order. No game:all_countries pool is constructed.
fallout_event_build_initial_registry = {
	fallout_event_reset_uncommitted_registry = yes
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.registry_building }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.version }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_schema = global.fallout_survival_ledger_schema_version }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_generation = global.fallout_survival_ledger_generation }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_survival_country_count = global.fallout_survival_ledger_country_count }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_ticket_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_next_ticket_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_ticket_id }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_last_broadcast_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_generation = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_broadcast_commit_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_global_flag = fallout_event_global_broadcast_receipt_initialized
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_review_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_source_count = global.fallout_successor_assigned_countries^num }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_registry_build_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.fallout_successor_assigned_countries
		set_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
		set_variable = { fallout_event_registry_generation = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_build_index }
		fallout_event_initialize_country_runtime = yes
		add_to_array = { array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries value = THIS }
		add_to_array = { array = global.fallout_event_registry_generation_entries value = global.fallout_event_registry_generation }
		add_to_array = { array = global.fallout_event_registry_index_entries value = fallout_event_registry_build_index }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_registry_build_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	}
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_count = global.fallout_event_registry_countries^num }
	fallout_event_promote_registry_empty_ordinary_receipts = yes
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_initialized_date = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_initialized_day = global.num_days }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_registry_payload_is_current = yes }
		set_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
		clr_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.orientation_pending }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.blocked }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.registry_alignment_failed }
		fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY
fallout_event_update_timeline_phase = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_map_return_receipts_are_current = yes }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days < constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.timeline_receipt_missing }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.second_world_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.open_continuation }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.soot_retreat_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.second_world }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.new_states_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.soot_retreat }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.rival_orders_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.new_states }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.consolidation_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.rival_orders }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.first_winter_year_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.consolidation }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.first_season_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_winter_year }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_timeline_elapsed_days > constant:fallout_event_timeline.ash_week_end_days } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_season }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_current_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.ash_week }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_phase_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_phase_last_update_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Bounded dormant transaction reconciliation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. One fixed ordinary opening receipt is reconciled
# before compact transaction rows. It writes only a data envelope and leaves
# dynamic event issuance to the single host-owned coordinator.
fallout_event_reconcile_ordinary_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_ordinary_target_type }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_ordinary_target }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_ordinary_target_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						}
						fallout_event_subject_is_current = yes
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_ordinary_target_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { exists = yes } }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_ordinary_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.owner_lost }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_cancel_ordinary_receipt = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_ordinary_target_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_ordinary_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.target_lost }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_cancel_ordinary_receipt = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_due_day value = global.num_days compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.ordinary_event }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_ordinary_ticket }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_ordinary_ai_event_token }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_ordinary_human_event_token }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
					fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch = fallout_event_ordinary_branch }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type = fallout_event_ordinary_target_type }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target = fallout_event_ordinary_target }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
					fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

fallout_event_advance_arc_reconcile_cursor = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor value = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
	}
}

fallout_event_advance_delayed_reconcile_cursor = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor value = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
	}
}

fallout_event_advance_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor value = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. The Year Zero pilot is the first authored major-arc
# stage consumer. It writes only after production approval and leaves ordinary
# receipt ownership untouched.
fallout_event_schedule_year_zero_arc_opening_dispatch = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_major_arc_stage_dispatch_attempted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_transaction_production_is_approved = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.opening }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_identity_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.year_zero_candidate_id }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
			}
			has_variable = fallout_event_401_candidate_state_id
			fallout_event_401_country_is_current = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_major_arc_stage_dispatch_attempted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.major_arc_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = constant:fallout_event_id.year_zero_human }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = constant:fallout_event_id.year_zero_ai }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.state }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target = fallout_event_401_candidate_state_id }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.arc_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. One compact arc row is selected. Its identity proof
# scans the capped local arc ledger for duplicate tickets, identities, or actors.
fallout_event_reconcile_one_arc_row = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_validation_index = fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_arc_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_arc_actor_type_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_arc_actor_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { exists = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.owner_lost }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				fallout_event_cancel_major_arc = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_subject_is_current = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.actor_lost }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				fallout_event_cancel_major_arc = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.opening } }
				fallout_event_schedule_year_zero_arc_opening_dispatch = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup } }
				set_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_pending
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.arc_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
		fallout_event_advance_arc_reconcile_cursor = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. One delayed row is selected. Structural validation
# scans local delayed identities before a due row may write a data-only envelope.
fallout_event_reconcile_one_delayed_row = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_status = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.result_scheduled }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.callback_scheduled }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_delayed_target_type_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_delayed_target_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_target_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
								check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
							}
							fallout_event_subject_is_current = yes
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_target_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { exists = yes } }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_requested_delayed_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.owner_lost }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						fallout_event_cancel_delayed_result = yes
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_target_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_requested_delayed_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.target_lost }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						fallout_event_cancel_delayed_result = yes
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_due_day_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor value = global.num_days compare = less_than_or_equals }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.delayed_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_delayed_ai_event_token_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_delayed_human_event_token_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
						fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch = fallout_event_delayed_branch_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type = fallout_event_delayed_target_type_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target = fallout_event_delayed_target_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
						fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.resolved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cancelled }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_is_deferred = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_prepare_delayed_cleanup = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_delayed_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cleanup_pending } }
				set_country_flag = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_pending
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.delayed_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_cleanup }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type = fallout_event_delayed_target_type_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target = fallout_event_delayed_target_entries^fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
		fallout_event_advance_delayed_reconcile_cursor = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: current bilateral reconcile index, requested ticket, and cancellation
# reason. Reconciliation cannot terminalize a row whose response event is
# already in the engine queue.
fallout_event_cancel_orphaned_bilateral_row = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_orphan_cancellation_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
	}
	fallout_event_cancel_local_bilateral_row_internal = yes
}

# Internal local terminalizer for a pair whose reciprocal proof has already
# failed. Inputs: row index, ticket, cancellation reason, issued authorization,
# and expected event token. The exact issued wrapper is the only caller allowed
# to set the authorization token.
fallout_event_cancel_local_bilateral_row_internal = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_transition_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_transition_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_orphan_cancellation_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				fallout_event_issued_bilateral_response_is_current = yes
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_transition_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_partner_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
		}
		var:fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = {
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_partner_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_partner_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_transition_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_transition_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_status = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_outcome = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_cancellation = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
		set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
					check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.bilateral_response }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
					OR = {
						fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_absent = yes
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
				}
				fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.bilateral }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
			fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_outcome }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_cancellation }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_status }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. One bilateral row is selected. Structural validation
# scans local identities and the exact reciprocal country before any mutation.
# Only a responder row can publish a response envelope.
fallout_event_reconcile_one_bilateral_row = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_has_reciprocal = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_has_reciprocal = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
					}
					NOT = { exists = yes }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.owner_lost }
				fallout_event_cancel_orphaned_bilateral_row = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
					}
					check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_has_reciprocal = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.reciprocal_lost }
				fallout_event_cancel_orphaned_bilateral_row = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_has_reciprocal = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_role.responder }
					exists = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_due_date_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor value = global.num_days compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.bilateral_response }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_bilateral_ai_response_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_bilateral_response_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
					fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch = fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.country }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
					fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_has_reciprocal = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_prepare_bilateral_cleanup = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_status = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
					if = {
						limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
						set_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending
					}
					else = {
						set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = fallout_event_bilateral_orphan_previous_status }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
						fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_reconciled_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending } }
				set_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.bilateral_response }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_cleanup }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_event_token = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_branch = fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.country }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_dispatch_target = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_write_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					fallout_event_write_dispatch_envelope = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
		fallout_event_advance_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: one country selected from the frozen registry. Repeated
# calls on the same date are idempotent even if a caller bypasses the global
# coordinator guard.
fallout_event_reconcile_country_transactions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = fallout_event_last_transaction_reconcile_generation }
				NOT = { has_variable = fallout_event_last_transaction_reconcile_day }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_last_transaction_reconcile_generation value = fallout_event_runtime_generation compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_last_transaction_reconcile_day value = global.num_days compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		fallout_event_reconcile_ordinary_receipt = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
			fallout_event_reconcile_one_arc_row = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
			fallout_event_reconcile_one_delayed_row = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
			fallout_event_reconcile_one_bilateral_row = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
				fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_event_last_transaction_reconcile_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_last_transaction_reconcile_day = global.num_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.country_row_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			NOT = { fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.country_row_mismatch }
		fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. One frozen registry country is selected
# per date. A reciprocal bilateral row may be updated inside that transaction.
fallout_event_reconcile_next_registry_country = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_registry_header_is_current = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_registry_reconcile_index = global.fallout_event_registry_cursor }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_registry_reconcile_country_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_registry_reconcile_index = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_registry_reconcile_country_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			fallout_event_promote_empty_ordinary_receipt = yes
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes }
				fallout_event_reconcile_country_transactions = yes
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_issue_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				fallout_event_issue_dispatch_envelope = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_registry_reconcile_country_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.fallout_event_registry_cursor value = global.fallout_event_registry_count compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_registry_reconcile_country_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.country_row_mismatch }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.invalid_cursor }
		fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. This coordinator hook performs dormant
# initialization, phase-receipt maintenance, schema promotion, bounded
# transaction reconciliation, and exact one-time dispatch issuance.
fallout_event_scheduler_reconcile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = fallout_active
			has_global_flag = fallout_transition_complete
			NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
				check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_ready }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			fallout_event_promote_registry_empty_ordinary_receipts = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_registry_ready
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			fallout_event_repromote_old_ready_empty_ordinary_receipts = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_event_last_broadcast_day }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_last_broadcast_day = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day }
			set_global_flag = fallout_event_global_broadcast_receipt_initialized
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_event_review_cursor }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_review_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
						check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.previous_initialization_only_version }
					}
					AND = {
						has_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version
						check_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.previous_without_survival_binding }
					}
				}
				NOT = { fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.survival_ledger_pending }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.survival_ledger_not_current }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
				check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.previous_initialization_only_version }
				fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			fallout_event_migrate_dormant_transaction_schema = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				has_variable = global.fallout_event_schema_version
				has_variable = global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version
				check_variable = { global.fallout_event_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_schema.version }
				check_variable = { global.fallout_event_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_event_registry_schema.previous_without_survival_binding }
				fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			fallout_event_migrate_registry_survival_binding = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
				NOT = { fallout_survival_ledger_header_is_current = yes }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
			}
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.survival_ledger_pending }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.survival_ledger_not_current }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_map_return_receipts_are_current = yes }
			fallout_event_update_timeline_phase = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
					NOT = { fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
				}
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.survival_ledger_pending }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.survival_ledger_not_current }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
					NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
					fallout_successor_allocation_is_current = yes
					fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes
				}
				fallout_event_build_initial_registry = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
					has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
					NOT = { fallout_event_registry_is_current = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.blocked }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.registry_source_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending
					NOT = { fallout_successor_allocation_is_current = yes }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready }
				}
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.blocked }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.allocation_not_current }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_initialization_pending }
			set_variable = { global.fallout_event_initialization_status = constant:fallout_event_initialization_status.blocked }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.legacy_timeline_unresolved }
			fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_error }
				has_global_flag = fallout_event_scheduler_registry_ready
			}
			fallout_event_migrate_current_empty_transaction_schema = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_global_flag = fallout_event_numerical_receipt_registry_ready }
					fallout_event_registry_header_is_current = yes
					fallout_successor_allocation_is_current = yes
					fallout_survival_ledger_is_current = yes
				}
				fallout_event_promote_registry_numerical_receipts = yes
			}
			fallout_event_reconcile_next_registry_country = yes
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_numerical_registry_header_is_current = yes }
				fallout_event_build_pilot_candidate_registries = yes
				fallout_event_review_human_successors = yes
				fallout_event_review_ai_registry_batch = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record.
fallout_event_record_orientation_component = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			NOT = { fallout_event_orientation_is_complete = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_national_complete
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_national_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition } }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_capital_complete
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_capital_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_resource_complete
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_resource_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_archetype_complete
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_archetype_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution } }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_character_complete
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_character_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_event_orientation_national_receipt_is_current = yes
				fallout_event_orientation_capital_receipt_is_current = yes
				fallout_event_orientation_resource_receipt_is_current = yes
				fallout_event_orientation_archetype_receipt_is_current = yes
				fallout_event_orientation_character_receipt_is_current = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_orientation_complete
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_status = constant:fallout_event_orientation_status.complete }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days.
fallout_event_apply_requested_ordinary_cooldown = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_transaction_production_is_approved = yes
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_due_day = global.num_days }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_due_day = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_due_day
					value = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_due_day
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_due_day = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_due_day }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: fallout_event_requested_ticket.
fallout_event_find_arc_ticket_index = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_find_break = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		array = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries
		value = fallout_event_stored_ticket
		index = fallout_event_stored_index
		break = fallout_event_find_break
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_stored_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_stored_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_find_break = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: fallout_event_transaction_row_index and fallout_event_requested_ticket.
fallout_event_remove_arc_row_at_index = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_identity_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_generation_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_primary_family_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cooldown_family_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_actor_type_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_actor_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_owner_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_visible_budget_cost_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
