		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor value = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
		fallout_event_refresh_arc_slot_flags = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: fallout_event_transaction_row_index and fallout_event_requested_ticket.
fallout_event_remove_delayed_row_at_index = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_key_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_generation_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_parent_arc_ticket_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_human_event_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_ai_event_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_branch_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_due_day_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_visible_budget_cost_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_target_type_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_target_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_owner_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor value = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
				check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.delayed_ticket }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
			}
			fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_of = {
						array = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries
						value = fallout_event_stored_status
						check_variable = { fallout_event_stored_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cleanup_pending }
					}
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_pending
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: row index and requested ticket. An issued cleanup also requires the
# exact authorization and expected event token before deletion can begin.
fallout_event_remove_bilateral_row_at_index = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_removal_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_removal_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_cleanup_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				fallout_event_issued_bilateral_cleanup_is_current = yes
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_removal_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_removal_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_key_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_generation_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_response_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ai_response_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_due_date_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_visible_budget_cost_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_owner_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_parent_arc_ticket_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor value = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_reconcile_cursor = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
				check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.bilateral_response }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
				fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_absent = yes
			}
			fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_of = {
						array = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries
						value = fallout_event_stored_status
						check_variable = { fallout_event_stored_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending }
					}
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ordinary opening reservation, cancellation, and atomic consumption
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs are the selected candidate request fields
# plus fallout_event_candidate_validation_index. The major branch reserves the
# existing capped arc row and waits for an authored stage consumer.
fallout_event_schedule_selected_major_arc_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc }
			fallout_event_candidate_major_arc_payload_is_current = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_arc_identity = fallout_event_requested_candidate_id }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token = fallout_event_requested_candidate_id }
		fallout_event_reserve_major_arc = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs are the selected candidate request fields
# plus fallout_event_candidate_validation_index. The relationship branch is
# intentionally dormant until the reciprocal candidate trigger proves every
# participant field. It writes only through the bilateral reservation API.
fallout_event_schedule_selected_relationship_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_relationship_payload_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship }
			fallout_event_candidate_relationship_payload_is_current = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_partner_index_to_check = fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index }
		var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_partner_index_to_check = {
			ROOT = {
				save_event_target_as = fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_response_token = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_ai_response_token = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = fallout_event_requested_control_mode }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token = fallout_event_requested_candidate_id }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
			}
			for_each_loop = {
				array = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries
				value = fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_id
				index = fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_id value = ROOT.fallout_event_requested_candidate_id compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_transaction_key_entries^fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index value = ROOT.fallout_event_requested_transaction_key compare = equals }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_class_entries^fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_bilateral_opportunity_entries^fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
					ROOT = {
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_relationship_payload_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_response_token = fallout_event_candidate_human_event_token_entries^fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_ai_response_token = fallout_event_candidate_ai_event_token_entries^fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost = fallout_event_candidate_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_relationship_partner_candidate_index }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						ROOT = {
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						}
					}
					else = {
						ROOT = { set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_relationship_payload_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			fallout_event_reserve_bilateral_transaction = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: transaction key, distinct human and AI
# event tokens, opening branch, due day, primary family, cooldown family, and
# optional typed target. Outputs: accepted, created, and ticket receipts.
fallout_event_schedule_ordinary_receipt = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_created = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_transaction_production_is_approved = yes
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
			fallout_event_ticket_ledger_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_transaction_key > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_human_event_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_branch > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_due_day value = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_registry_index value = fallout_event_registry_index compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_issue_day value = fallout_event_requested_due_day compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_issue_day value = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_primary_family > constant:fallout_event_primary_family.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_primary_family < constant:fallout_event_primary_family.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family > constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family < constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_phase > constant:fallout_event_phase.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_phase < constant:fallout_event_phase.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class > constant:fallout_event_class.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class < constant:fallout_event_class.upper_bound }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.crisis_incident }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.routine_incident }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.broadcast }
			}
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_mode < constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.upper_bound }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_mode < constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.none } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_break_used < constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.upper_bound }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_crisis_break_used < constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.none } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_partner_index_to_check = fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship }
					fallout_event_partner_registry_index_is_current = yes
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_class = constant:fallout_event_class.relationship } }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_candidate_id > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_score > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_visible_budget_cost } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index < constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket < constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_actor_type < constant:fallout_event_subject_type.upper_bound }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_actor_type < constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_repeatable < constant:fallout_event_repeatability.upper_bound }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_repeatable < constant:fallout_event_repeatability.nonrepeatable } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_route < constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
			}
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_key_entries value = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key } }
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_key_entries value = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_requested_target_type }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_requested_target }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					}
					fallout_event_subject_shape_is_valid = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				has_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_transaction_key value = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_human_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_ai_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_branch value = fallout_event_requested_branch compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_due_day value = fallout_event_requested_due_day compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_registry_index value = fallout_event_requested_registry_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_issue_day value = fallout_event_requested_issue_day compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_primary_family value = fallout_event_requested_primary_family compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_family value = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_phase value = fallout_event_requested_phase compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_class value = fallout_event_requested_class compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_crisis_mode value = fallout_event_requested_crisis_mode compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_crisis_break_used value = fallout_event_requested_crisis_break_used compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_candidate_id value = fallout_event_requested_candidate_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_score value = fallout_event_requested_score compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_visible_budget_cost value = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index value = fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_parent_arc_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_actor_type value = fallout_event_requested_actor_type compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_actor value = fallout_event_requested_actor compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_repeatable value = fallout_event_requested_repeatable compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_route value = fallout_event_requested_route compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_control_mode value = fallout_event_requested_control_mode compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_target_type value = fallout_event_requested_target_type compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_target value = fallout_event_requested_target compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = fallout_event_ordinary_ticket }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_due_day value = global.num_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_due_day value = global.num_days compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_issue_day value = global.num_days compare = equals }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					}
					fallout_event_subject_is_current = yes
				}
			}
			fallout_event_allocate_transaction_ticket = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_transaction_key = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_human_event_token = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_ai_event_token = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_branch = fallout_event_requested_branch }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_due_day = fallout_event_requested_due_day }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_registry_index = fallout_event_requested_registry_index }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_issue_day = fallout_event_requested_issue_day }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_primary_family = fallout_event_requested_primary_family }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_cooldown_family = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_target_type = fallout_event_requested_target_type }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_target = fallout_event_requested_target }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_phase = fallout_event_requested_phase }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_class = fallout_event_requested_class }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_crisis_mode = fallout_event_requested_crisis_mode }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_crisis_break_used = fallout_event_requested_crisis_break_used }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_candidate_id = fallout_event_requested_candidate_id }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_score = fallout_event_requested_score }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_visible_budget_cost = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_partner_registry_index = fallout_event_requested_partner_registry_index }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_parent_arc_ticket = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_actor_type = fallout_event_requested_actor_type }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_actor = fallout_event_requested_actor }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_repeatable = fallout_event_requested_repeatable }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_route = fallout_event_requested_route }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_ordinary_control_mode = fallout_event_requested_control_mode }
				set_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_created = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
				else = {
					fallout_event_clear_ordinary_receipt = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.ordinary_receipt_mismatch }
					fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: requested ticket and cancellation reason.
# This never clears an issued engine event. Exact retries are source-bound to
# the generation-bound cancellation receipt.
fallout_event_cancel_ordinary_receipt = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
			has_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_ordinary_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
			NOT = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
				check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.ordinary_event }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
			}
			fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.ordinary_event }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
		fallout_event_clear_ordinary_receipt = yes
		fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
				fallout_event_transaction_history_receipts_are_consistent = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_pending }
			has_variable = fallout_event_last_cancelled_ticket
			has_variable = fallout_event_last_cancellation_source
			has_variable = fallout_event_last_cancellation_reason
			has_variable = fallout_event_last_cancellation_generation
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_last_cancelled_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_last_cancellation_source = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.ordinary_event }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_last_cancellation_reason value = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_last_cancellation_generation value = fallout_event_runtime_generation compare = equals }
			fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: expected event token and cancellation
# reason. This is the only cancellation path for an ordinary event already
# issued by the engine. Event content must revalidate its target before calling
# this wrapper. The active event clears its envelope before releasing the fixed
# receipt.
fallout_event_cancel_issued_ordinary_receipt = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_has_issued_dispatch = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.ordinary_event }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
		fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
		fallout_event_clear_ordinary_receipt = yes
		fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes
				fallout_event_dispatch_envelope_is_consistent = yes
				fallout_event_transaction_history_receipts_are_consistent = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: expected event token. This consumes only the
# exact issued ordinary opening. It clears the envelope first, then the fixed
# receipt, so no stale transaction can authenticate a second delivery.
fallout_event_consume_issued_ordinary_receipt = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_ordinary_receipt_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
		}
		fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
		fallout_event_clear_ordinary_receipt = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_ordinary_receipt_is_consistent = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. This consumes the exact major-arc opening
# envelope while retaining the capped arc row for stage advancement and
# content-owned cleanup.
fallout_event_consume_issued_major_arc_stage = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_major_arc_stage_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
		}
		fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_dispatch_envelope_is_consistent = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Major-arc reservation, advancement, cancellation, and cleanup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Inputs: fallout_event_requested_arc_identity, requested primary and cooldown
# families, requested actor type/value, and requested cleanup token. Outputs:
# fallout_event_transaction_accepted and fallout_event_transaction_ticket.
fallout_event_reserve_major_arc = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_transaction_production_is_approved = yes
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_arc_receipts_are_current = yes
			fallout_event_arc_all_row_identities_are_unique = yes
			fallout_event_ticket_ledger_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_arc_identity > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_primary_family > constant:fallout_event_primary_family.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_primary_family < constant:fallout_event_primary_family.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family > constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family < constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.upper_bound }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_visible_budget_cost } }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
				}
			}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_requested_actor_type }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_requested_actor }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_subject_shape_is_valid = yes }
			fallout_event_find_arc_identity_index = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_primary_family_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_primary_family compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_cooldown_family_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_actor_type_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_actor_type compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_actor_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_actor compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_control_mode compare = equals }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index < constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_active_major_arc_count < constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_major_arcs }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
							is_ai = no
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					fallout_event_subject_is_current = yes
					NOT = {
						any_of = {
							array = fallout_event_arc_actor_entries
							value = fallout_event_stored_actor
							index = fallout_event_stored_actor_index
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_stored_actor value = fallout_event_requested_actor compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_actor_type_entries^fallout_event_stored_actor_index value = fallout_event_requested_actor_type compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
				fallout_event_allocate_transaction_ticket = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries^num }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_identity_entries value = fallout_event_requested_arc_identity }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_generation_entries value = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_primary_family_entries value = fallout_event_requested_primary_family }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cooldown_family_entries value = fallout_event_requested_cooldown_family }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_actor_type_entries value = fallout_event_requested_actor_type }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_actor_entries value = fallout_event_requested_actor }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries value = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries value = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_owner_entries value = fallout_event_registry_index }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_visible_budget_cost_entries value = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_control_mode_entries value = fallout_event_requested_control_mode }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries value = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.opening }
					fallout_event_refresh_arc_slot_flags = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
					if = {
						limit = { fallout_event_arc_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
						fallout_event_remove_arc_row_at_index = yes
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.arc_receipt_mismatch }
						fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: fallout_event_requested_ticket, fallout_event_requested_arc_stage,
# and fallout_event_requested_outcome.
fallout_event_advance_major_arc = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_arc_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_arc_stage > constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_arc_stage < constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.closed }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome < constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome < constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
		}
		fallout_event_find_arc_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_expected_next_arc_stage = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_event_expected_next_arc_stage = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_arc_stage compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_outcome compare = equals }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_arc_stage value = fallout_event_expected_next_arc_stage compare = equals }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_outcome compare = equals }
						}
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_arc_stage < constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome > constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_previous_stage = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_previous_outcome = fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_previous_cancellation = fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_arc_stage }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_arc_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_arc_stage = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup } }
					set_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_pending
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_previous_outcome }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_previous_cancellation }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_previous_stage }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.arc_receipt_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: fallout_event_requested_ticket and cancellation reason.
fallout_event_cancel_major_arc = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_arc_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
		}
		fallout_event_find_arc_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index < constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index < constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.delayed_result } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_previous_stage = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_previous_outcome = fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_previous_cancellation = fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_arc_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
				set_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_pending
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.major_arc }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
				fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_previous_outcome }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_previous_cancellation }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_arc_previous_stage }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.arc_receipt_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
				}
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason compare = equals }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_arc_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: fallout_event_requested_ticket and fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token.
# The content-owned cleanup effect must call this only after clearing its memory.
fallout_event_release_major_arc = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_arc_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		fallout_event_find_arc_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_stage_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_arc_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index > constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token compare = equals }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_arc_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_arc_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			fallout_event_remove_arc_row_at_index = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = fallout_event_arc_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_requested_ticket } } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						any_of = {
							array = fallout_event_arc_stage_entries
							value = fallout_event_stored_stage
							check_variable = { fallout_event_stored_stage = constant:fallout_event_arc_stage.cleanup }
						}
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = fallout_event_arc_cleanup_pending
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Delayed-result reservation, terminalization, and cleanup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Inputs: transaction key, optional parent arc ticket, human and AI event
# tokens, branch, due day, queue status, optional target type/value, and cleanup
# token. Outputs: fallout_event_transaction_accepted and transaction ticket.
fallout_event_schedule_delayed_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_transaction_production_is_approved = yes
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_delayed_queue_receipts_are_current = yes
			fallout_event_delayed_all_row_identities_are_unique = yes
			fallout_event_arc_receipts_are_current = yes
			fallout_event_ticket_ledger_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_transaction_key > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			fallout_event_requested_key_differs_from_ordinary_receipt = yes
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket < constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_human_event_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_branch > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_due_day value = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_queue_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.result_scheduled }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_queue_status = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.callback_scheduled }
			}
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = fallout_event_requested_target_type }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = fallout_event_requested_target }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_type_to_check = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_subject_value_to_check = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					}
					fallout_event_subject_shape_is_valid = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_target_shape_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			fallout_event_find_delayed_key_index = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_generation_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_runtime_generation compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_parent_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_human_event_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_ai_event_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_branch_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_branch compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_due_day_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_due_day compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_control_mode compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_queue_status compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_target_type_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_target_type compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_target_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_target compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_owner_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_registry_index compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num < constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_delayed_rows }
					fallout_event_requested_delayed_new_row_inputs_are_current = yes
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
							is_ai = no
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
				}
				fallout_event_allocate_transaction_ticket = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries^num }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_key_entries value = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_generation_entries value = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_parent_arc_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_human_event_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_human_event_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_ai_event_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_ai_event_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_branch_entries value = fallout_event_requested_branch }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_due_day_entries value = fallout_event_requested_due_day }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_visible_budget_cost_entries value = fallout_event_requested_visible_budget_cost }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_control_mode_entries value = fallout_event_requested_control_mode }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_target_type_entries value = fallout_event_requested_target_type }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_target_entries value = fallout_event_requested_target }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_owner_entries value = fallout_event_registry_index }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries value = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries value = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries value = fallout_event_requested_queue_status }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
					if = {
						limit = { fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
							fallout_event_calculate_base_cooldown_days = yes
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days = fallout_event_base_cooldown_days }
							fallout_event_apply_requested_ordinary_cooldown = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
						fallout_event_remove_delayed_row_at_index = yes
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
						fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: requested ticket and non-zero outcome. This
# public path accepts only an unissued row. An issued engine event must use the
# exact token-authenticated wrapper below.
fallout_event_resolve_delayed_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_requested_delayed_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
		fallout_event_resolve_delayed_result_internal = yes
	}
}

# Internal terminalizer. Call only from the unissued gate above or from the
# exact issued wrapper after token authentication.
fallout_event_resolve_delayed_result_internal = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_delayed_queue_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome > constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome < constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
		}
		fallout_event_find_delayed_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.result_scheduled }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.callback_scheduled }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_previous_status = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.resolved }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
						check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.delayed_ticket }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
					}
					fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_delayed_previous_status }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.resolved }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_outcome compare = equals }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: requested ticket, cancellation reason, and
# either cancelled outcome. This public path accepts only an unissued row.
fallout_event_cancel_delayed_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_requested_delayed_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
		fallout_event_cancel_delayed_result_internal = yes
	}
}

# Internal terminalizer. Call only from the unissued gate above or from the
# exact issued wrapper after token authentication.
fallout_event_cancel_delayed_result_internal = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_delayed_queue_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
			}
		}
		fallout_event_find_delayed_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.result_scheduled }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.callback_scheduled }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_previous_status = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cancelled }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
						check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.delayed_ticket }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
					}
					fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.delayed_result }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
				fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_delayed_previous_status }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cancelled }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_outcome_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_outcome compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason compare = equals }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Input: requested ticket. Terminal status is retained until this explicit
# handoff marks the content-owned cleanup transaction ready.
fallout_event_prepare_delayed_cleanup = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_delayed_queue_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		}
		fallout_event_find_delayed_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.resolved }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cancelled }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_previous_status = fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cleanup_pending }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes }
				set_country_flag = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_pending
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_delayed_previous_status }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.delayed_receipt_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cleanup_pending }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_pending
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: requested ticket and exact cleanup token.
# This public path accepts only an unissued cleanup row.
fallout_event_release_delayed_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_requested_delayed_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
		fallout_event_release_delayed_result_internal = yes
	}
}

# Internal remover. Call only from the unissued gate above or from the exact
# issued cleanup wrapper after the content-owned memories are cleared.
fallout_event_release_delayed_result_internal = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_delayed_queue_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		fallout_event_find_delayed_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_queue_status.cleanup_pending }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_delayed_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token compare = equals }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_delayed_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
