				fallout_event_delayed_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			fallout_event_remove_delayed_row_at_index = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = fallout_event_delayed_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_requested_ticket } } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: expected event token and non-zero outcome.
# The wrapper authenticates the issued receipt before delegating to the row
# terminalizer. The terminalizer consumes the matching envelope atomically, so
# callers must not acknowledge it a second time.
fallout_event_resolve_issued_delayed_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_delayed_result_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome > constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome < constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		fallout_event_resolve_delayed_result_internal = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: expected event token, cancellation reason,
# and either cancelled outcome. This is the issued-event stale-path consumer.
fallout_event_cancel_issued_delayed_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_delayed_result_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		fallout_event_cancel_delayed_result_internal = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: expected cleanup event token. The wrapper
# derives the exact ticket and cleanup token from the issued receipt, removes
# the matching row, and consumes the envelope in the same transaction.
fallout_event_release_issued_delayed_cleanup = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_delayed_cleanup_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token }
		fallout_event_release_delayed_result_internal = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Reciprocal bilateral reservation, transition, and cleanup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: requested ticket. Outputs the pair-current
# flag, both row indexes, and short-lived origin and partner event targets.
fallout_event_load_current_bilateral_pair = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_bilateral_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
		}
		fallout_event_find_bilateral_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
				save_event_target_as = fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_origin
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				var:fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					save_event_target_as = fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
					event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
						fallout_event_find_bilateral_ticket_index = yes
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index }
							if = {
								limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
								set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Inputs: short-lived event target fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner,
# transaction key, participant-specific human and AI response tokens, due day,
# optional parent arc ticket, and cleanup token. Outputs acceptance and ticket.
fallout_event_reserve_bilateral_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner
			fallout_event_transaction_production_is_approved = yes
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_bilateral_receipts_are_current = yes
			fallout_event_bilateral_all_row_identities_are_unique = yes
			fallout_event_arc_receipts_are_current = yes
			fallout_event_ticket_ledger_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_transaction_key > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			fallout_event_requested_key_differs_from_ordinary_receipt = yes
			event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
				fallout_event_requested_key_differs_from_ordinary_receipt = yes
			}
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_response_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_ai_response_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_initiator_response_token value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_ai_response_token compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_response_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_ai_response_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_responder_response_token value = fallout_event_requested_responder_ai_response_token compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_requested_due_day value = global.fallout_event_timeline_start_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket < constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				}
			}
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_visible_budget_cost } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost > constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_visible_budget_cost } }
		}
		save_event_target_as = fallout_event_bilateral_initiator
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_has_capacity = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_control_mode_matches = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
					fallout_event_bilateral_receipts_are_current = yes
					fallout_event_bilateral_all_row_identities_are_unique = yes
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_registry_index compare = equals } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num < constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_bilateral_rows }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_has_capacity = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
								is_ai = no
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
								is_ai = yes
							}
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_control_mode_matches = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			fallout_event_find_bilateral_key_index = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_found = fallout_event_transaction_row_found }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
				fallout_event_find_bilateral_key_index = yes
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_found = fallout_event_transaction_row_found }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_existing_bilateral_ticket = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_exact_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				if = {
					limit = {
						fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_role.initiator }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_response_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_response_token compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_ai_response_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_ai_response_token compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_due_date_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_due_day compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_parent_arc_ticket_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token compare = equals }
						var:fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index = {
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_exact_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_registry_index compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index value = fallout_event_existing_bilateral_ticket compare = equals }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_role.responder }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_response_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_responder_response_token compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_ai_response_token_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_responder_ai_response_token compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_visible_budget_cost_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_index value = fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode compare = equals }
						}
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_exact_partner_context_loaded = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_index }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
			}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_key_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_key_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num < constant:fallout_event_runtime.maximum_bilateral_rows }
						fallout_event_requested_bilateral_new_row_inputs_are_current = yes
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_has_capacity = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible }
							is_ai = no
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai }
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_control_mode_matches = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				}
				fallout_event_allocate_transaction_ticket = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_append_index = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries value = event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_key_entries value = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_generation_entries value = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries value = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_role.initiator }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_response_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_response_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ai_response_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_ai_response_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_due_date_entries value = fallout_event_requested_due_day }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_visible_budget_cost_entries value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_visible_budget_cost }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries value = fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_owner_entries value = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_registry_index }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_parent_arc_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries value = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries value = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
					add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries value = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
					event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_append_index = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries^num }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_entries value = event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_initiator }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_key_entries value = fallout_event_requested_transaction_key }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_generation_entries value = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_role_entries value = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_role.responder }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_response_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_responder_response_token }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ai_response_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_responder_ai_response_token }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_due_date_entries value = fallout_event_requested_due_day }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_visible_budget_cost_entries value = fallout_event_requested_responder_visible_budget_cost }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_control_mode_entries value = fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_owner_entries value = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_registry_index }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_parent_arc_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_requested_parent_arc_ticket }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries value = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries value = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
						add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries value = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_commit_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_append_index }
					if = {
						limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
						event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_append_index }
							if = {
								limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
								set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_commit_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_commit_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_initiator_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
							fallout_event_calculate_base_cooldown_days = yes
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days = fallout_event_base_cooldown_days }
							fallout_event_apply_requested_ordinary_cooldown = yes
						}
						event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_responder_control_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
								set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_base_cooldown_days = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
								fallout_event_calculate_base_cooldown_days = yes
								set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cooldown_days = fallout_event_base_cooldown_days }
								fallout_event_apply_requested_ordinary_cooldown = yes
							}
						}
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
					else = {
						event_target:fallout_event_requested_bilateral_partner = {
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_append_index }
							set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
							fallout_event_remove_bilateral_row_at_index = yes
						}
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_append_index }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_transaction_ticket }
						fallout_event_remove_bilateral_row_at_index = yes
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
						fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: requested ticket. Outputs the loaded pair
# receipts plus a flag proving neither country has issued this ticket.
fallout_event_load_unissued_bilateral_pair = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	fallout_event_load_current_bilateral_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
}

# Internal pair terminalizer. Inputs: requested ticket, status, outcome,
# cancellation reason, issued authorization, and expected event token. Both
# rows commit before an authenticated response envelope is cleared.
fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	fallout_event_load_current_bilateral_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_dispatch_is_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_dispatch_is_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					fallout_event_issued_bilateral_response_is_current = yes
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_dispatch_is_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_dispatch_is_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_previous_outcome = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_previous_cancellation = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
		event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_previous_status = fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_previous_outcome = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_previous_cancellation = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_dispatch_is_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_current_status value = fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_outcome compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_dispatch_is_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome > constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome < constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
					}
					AND = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.reserved }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
						}
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.cancelled_before_choice }
						}
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
					}
					AND = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_current_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
						}
						check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_outcome compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason compare = equals }
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_transition_is_valid = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_requested_outcome }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
			event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
				set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status }
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_commit_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
				event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index }
					if = {
						limit = { fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_has_current_reciprocal = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_commit_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_commit_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
							check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
							check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.bilateral_response }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
							OR = {
								fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_absent = yes
								check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
							}
						}
						fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
					}
					event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
						if = {
							limit = {
									has_country_flag = fallout_event_dispatch_ready
									check_variable = { fallout_event_dispatch_source = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_source.bilateral_response }
									check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_ticket value = fallout_event_requested_ticket compare = equals }
									fallout_event_dispatch_issue_receipt_is_absent = yes
								}
							fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancelled_ticket = fallout_event_requested_ticket }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_source_to_record = constant:fallout_event_transaction_source.bilateral }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_cancellation_reason_to_record = fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason }
					fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes
					event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = { fallout_event_record_cancellation = yes }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending } }
					set_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending
					event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = { set_country_flag = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_pending }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
					set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_previous_outcome }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_previous_cancellation }
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_previous_outcome }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_origin_previous_cancellation }
				event_target:fallout_event_bilateral_loaded_partner = {
					set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_partner_previous_status }
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = fallout_event_bilateral_current_status }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_scheduler_error_to_register = constant:fallout_event_scheduler_error.bilateral_reciprocal_mismatch }
				fallout_event_register_scheduler_error_if_unowned = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Input: requested ticket. This public route accepts only an unissued pair.
fallout_event_mark_bilateral_response_pending = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	fallout_event_load_unissued_bilateral_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.response_pending }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
		fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: requested ticket and non-zero outcome. This public route accepts only
# an unissued pair.
fallout_event_resolve_bilateral_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	fallout_event_load_unissued_bilateral_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
		fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: requested ticket, cancelled outcome, and cancellation reason. This
# public route accepts only an unissued pair.
fallout_event_cancel_bilateral_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	fallout_event_load_unissued_bilateral_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
		fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: expected event token and non-zero outcome.
# This atomically resolves both reciprocal rows and consumes the exact issued
# response envelope only after the pair commit proves current.
fallout_event_resolve_issued_bilateral_response = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_bilateral_response_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome > constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome < constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Inputs: expected event token, aborted outcome, and
# cancellation reason. A current reciprocal pair is cancelled atomically. If
# the reciprocal proof is already lost, only the authenticated local tombstone
# is terminalized.
fallout_event_cancel_issued_bilateral_response = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_bilateral_response_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.aborted_after_choice }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason > constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason < constant:fallout_event_cancellation_reason.upper_bound }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		fallout_event_load_current_bilateral_pair = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
			fallout_event_find_bilateral_ticket_index = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_orphan_cancellation_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				fallout_event_cancel_local_bilateral_row_internal = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Input: requested ticket. Outcome and reason are copied from the exact
# reciprocal terminal receipt before both unissued rows enter cleanup.
fallout_event_prepare_bilateral_cleanup = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_transition_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_has_issued_dispatch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_current = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_partner_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	fallout_event_load_unissued_bilateral_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_pair_is_unissued = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.resolved }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cancelled }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_status = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_outcome = fallout_event_bilateral_outcome_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cancellation_reason = fallout_event_bilateral_cancellation_reason_entries^fallout_event_bilateral_origin_row_index }
		fallout_event_update_bilateral_pair_internal = yes
	}
}

# Inputs: requested ticket and exact cleanup token. Each country releases its
# own unissued row only after its content-owned cleanup is complete.
fallout_event_release_bilateral_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_cleanup_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes } }
		fallout_event_release_bilateral_transaction_internal = yes
	}
}

# Internal local cleanup remover. The issued wrapper is the only caller allowed
# to provide cleanup authorization and an expected event token.
fallout_event_release_bilateral_transaction_internal = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_release_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_event_requested_bilateral_ticket_has_issued_dispatch = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_release_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_cleanup_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				fallout_event_issued_bilateral_cleanup_is_current = yes
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_release_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.first_array_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_release_allowed = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_bilateral_receipts_are_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }
			check_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
		}
		fallout_event_find_bilateral_ticket_index = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_row_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_status_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index = constant:fallout_event_bilateral_status.cleanup_pending }
				check_variable = { var = fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_token_entries^fallout_event_transaction_row_index value = fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token compare = equals }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_validation_index = fallout_event_transaction_row_index }
				fallout_event_bilateral_row_at_index_is_structurally_current = yes
			}
			fallout_event_remove_bilateral_row_at_index = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = fallout_event_bilateral_ticket_entries value = fallout_event_requested_ticket } } }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_cleanup_dispatch_clear_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one } }
					fallout_event_clear_dispatch_envelope = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input: expected cleanup event token. The wrapper
# derives the exact ticket and cleanup token, removes the local row, and consumes
# the issued cleanup envelope in one effect chain.
fallout_event_release_issued_bilateral_cleanup = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
			fallout_event_issued_bilateral_cleanup_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token value = fallout_event_requested_event_token compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_ticket = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_ticket }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_requested_cleanup_token = fallout_event_dispatch_issued_event_token }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_bilateral_issued_cleanup_authorized = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
		fallout_event_release_bilateral_transaction_internal = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dormant Ash-week Fallout orientation transaction substrate
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Clears only the derived resource pilot identity.
# The future reviewed asset package receipt is separate and is not reset here.
fallout_orientation_reset_resource_mapping = {
	clr_country_flag = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_mapped
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_schema_version
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_country_memory
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_region
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_archetype
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch
}

fallout_orientation_clear_resource_registry_diagnostic = {
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic_day
}

fallout_orientation_record_resource_registry_diagnostic = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic = fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic_generation = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic_day = global.num_days }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Builds one of twelve exact successor-memory rows.
# It chooses Fuel or Power but does not prove the capital asset, approve a
# coverage surface, issue an event, or activate the ordinary scheduler.
fallout_orientation_prepare_resource_mapping = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_is_current = yes }
		fallout_orientation_clear_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else = {
		fallout_orientation_reset_resource_mapping = yes
		fallout_orientation_clear_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_successor_assignment_country_row_is_current = yes }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_schema.version }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_country_memory = fallout_country_memory_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_region = fallout_region_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }

			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.federal_continuity_zone } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.north_america } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.continuity_government } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.ration } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.alpine_redoubt } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.europe } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.bunker_authority } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.rhine_dead_cities } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.europe } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.scavenger_syndicate } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.don_steppe_hetmanates } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.eurasian_interior } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.warlord_command } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.requisition } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.mekong_greenhouse_compact } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.east_asia } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.food_compact } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.bengal_delta_raft_republic } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.south_asia } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.maritime_remnant } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.levant_quarantine_cities } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.middle_east_north_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.quarantine_state } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.ration } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.congo_green_basin } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.mutant_polity } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.sahel_caravan_wards } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.nomad_convoy } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.ethiopian_highland_refuge } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.religious_refuge } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.ration } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.atacama_observatory_state } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.latin_america_caribbean } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.technate } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.antarctic_listening_government } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.oceania_remote_islands } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.machine_protocol } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.ration } }

			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource has_variable = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch }
				set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_mapped
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_is_current = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
				}
				else = {
					fallout_orientation_reset_resource_mapping = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic.payload_invalid }
					fallout_orientation_record_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic.identity_mismatch }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_country_memory_has_pilot_resource_mapping = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic.unsupported_country_memory }
				}
				fallout_orientation_reset_resource_mapping = yes
				fallout_orientation_record_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic.allocation_not_current }
			fallout_orientation_record_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Copies only a fully authenticated pilot package into
# the temporary resource request. A missing package writes an explicit empty
# support resource so caller data cannot bypass the reviewed evidence receipt.
fallout_orientation_load_resource_request = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_request_loaded = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	fallout_orientation_prepare_resource_mapping = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_tailored_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.none }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_resource_registry_is_usable = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_tailored_resource = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_support_resource }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_resource_request_loaded = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		fallout_orientation_clear_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic.package_not_current }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_is_current = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_registry_diagnostic.payload_invalid }
		}
		fallout_orientation_record_resource_registry_diagnostic = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Clears only the derived government pilot row.
fallout_orientation_reset_government_mapping = {
	clr_country_flag = fallout_orientation_government_registry_mapped
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_schema_version
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_country_memory
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_region
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_archetype
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_days
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_token
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch
}

fallout_orientation_clear_government_registry_diagnostic = {
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic_day
}

fallout_orientation_record_government_registry_diagnostic = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic = fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic_generation = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic_day = global.num_days }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Builds one exact reviewed country-memory row. It
# does not approve any orientation surface, issue an event, or call a scheduler.
fallout_orientation_prepare_government_mapping = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_government_mapping_is_current = yes }
		fallout_orientation_clear_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else = {
		fallout_orientation_reset_government_mapping = yes
		fallout_orientation_clear_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_successor_assignment_country_row_is_current = yes }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_schema.version }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_country_memory = fallout_country_memory_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_region = fallout_region_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_days = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }

			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.federal_continuity_zone } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.north_america } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.continuity_government } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.federal_continuity_zone } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.recognition } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.federal_recognition } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.alpine_redoubt } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.europe } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.bunker_authority } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.alpine_redoubt } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.recovery } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.alpine_recovery } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.rhine_dead_cities } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.europe } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.scavenger_syndicate } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.rhine_dead_cities } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.reclamation } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.rhine_reclamation } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.consolidate } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.don_steppe_hetmanates } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.eurasian_interior } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.warlord_command } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.don_steppe_hetmanates } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.supply } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.don_supply } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.rival } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.mekong_greenhouse_compact } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.east_asia } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.food_compact } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.mekong_greenhouse_compact } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.recovery } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.mekong_recovery } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.bengal_delta_raft_republic } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.south_asia } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.maritime_remnant } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.bengal_delta_raft_republic } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.supply } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.bengal_supply } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.levant_quarantine_cities } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.middle_east_north_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.quarantine_state } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.levant_quarantine_cities } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.recovery } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.levant_recovery } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
