			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.congo_green_basin } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.mutant_polity } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.congo_green_basin } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.cohesion } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.congo_cohesion } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.sahel_caravan_wards } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.nomad_convoy } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.sahel_caravan_wards } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.adaptation } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.sahel_adaptation } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.ethiopian_highland_refuge } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.religious_refuge } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.ethiopian_highland_refuge } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.cohesion } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.ethiopian_cohesion } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.atacama_observatory_state } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.latin_america_caribbean } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.technate } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.atacama_observatory_state } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.supply } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.atacama_supply } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.antarctic_listening_government } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.oceania_remote_islands } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.machine_protocol } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.antarctic_listening_government } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.adaptation } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_government_effect_value.antarctic_adaptation } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } }

			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id has_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind has_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value has_variable = fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch }
				set_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_token = fallout_orientation_government_registry_row_id }
				set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_government_registry_mapped
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_orientation_government_mapping_is_current = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
				}
				else = {
					fallout_orientation_reset_government_mapping = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic.payload_invalid }
					fallout_orientation_record_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic.identity_mismatch }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_country_memory_has_pilot_government_mapping = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic.unsupported_country_memory }
				}
				fallout_orientation_reset_government_mapping = yes
				fallout_orientation_record_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic.allocation_not_current }
			fallout_orientation_record_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Copies only an exact approved government row into
# the temporary request. This tranche provides no approval setter.
fallout_orientation_load_government_request = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_request_loaded = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	fallout_orientation_prepare_government_mapping = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_value = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_days = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_token = constant:fallout_orientation_government_row_id.none }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_government_registry_is_usable = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_kind = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_kind }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_value = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_value }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_days = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_days }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_token = fallout_orientation_government_registry_effect_token }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_government_request_loaded = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		fallout_orientation_clear_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic.approval_not_current }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_government_mapping_is_current = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_government_registry_diagnostic.payload_invalid }
		}
		fallout_orientation_record_government_registry_diagnostic = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Clears only the derived candidate identity mapping.
# Candidate package receipts belong to their future installer and are not reset
# here. A new transition generation makes those receipts stale automatically.
fallout_orientation_reset_candidate_mapping = {
	clr_country_flag = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_mapped
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_schema_version
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_country_memory
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_region
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_archetype
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_count
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_type
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_type
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_type
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_region
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_region
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_region
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_archetype
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_archetype
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_archetype
}

fallout_orientation_clear_candidate_registry_diagnostic = {
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic_generation
	clear_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic_day
}

fallout_orientation_record_candidate_registry_diagnostic = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic_generation = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic_day = global.num_days }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Builds one reviewed pilot mapping after successor
# allocation has committed the exact region, archetype, and country memory. It
# does not install candidates, approve package receipts, or activate orientation.
fallout_orientation_prepare_candidate_mapping = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_candidate_mapping_is_current = yes }
		fallout_orientation_clear_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else = {
		fallout_orientation_reset_candidate_mapping = yes
		fallout_orientation_clear_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_successor_assignment_country_row_is_current = yes }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_schema_version = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_schema.version }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_country_memory = fallout_country_memory_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_region = fallout_region_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_count = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.three }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.character }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.character }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.institution }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_region = fallout_region_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_region = fallout_region_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_region = fallout_region_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }

			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.federal_continuity_zone } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.north_america } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.continuity_government } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.federal_emergency_records_commissioner } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.federal_state_guard_supply_custodian } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.federal_emergency_congress_secretariat } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.alpine_redoubt } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.europe } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.bunker_authority } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.alpine_tunnel_medical_mediator } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.alpine_redoubt_quartermaster } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.alpine_glacier_water_board } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.rhine_dead_cities } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.europe } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.scavenger_syndicate } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.rhine_dead_city_permit_physician } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.rhine_factory_recovery_marshal } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.rhine_salvage_court } }

			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.sahel_caravan_wards } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.nomad_convoy } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.sahel_mobile_well_medic } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.sahel_well_guardian_escort_captain } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.sahel_caravan_court_radio_market_council } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.ethiopian_highland_refuge } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.sub_saharan_africa } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.religious_refuge } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.ethiopian_highland_storehouse_relief_coordinator } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.ethiopian_mountain_road_protection_captain } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.ethiopian_highland_sanctuary_council } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.atacama_observatory_state } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.latin_america_caribbean } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.technate } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.atacama_desert_pump_engineer } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.atacama_mine_port_supply_captain } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.atacama_observatory_data_council } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id = constant:fallout_country_memory.antarctic_listening_government } check_variable = { fallout_region_id = constant:fallout_region.oceania_remote_islands } check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.machine_protocol } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.antarctic_human_survival_records_officer } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.antarctic_station_maintenance_chief } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.antarctic_weather_rescue_protocol_council } }

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id
					has_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id
					has_variable = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id
				}
				set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_mapped
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_orientation_candidate_mapping_is_current = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_mapping_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
				}
				else = {
					fallout_orientation_reset_candidate_mapping = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.payload_invalid }
					fallout_orientation_record_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.identity_mismatch }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_country_memory_has_pilot_candidate_mapping = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.unsupported_country_memory }
				}
				fallout_orientation_reset_candidate_mapping = yes
				fallout_orientation_record_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.allocation_not_current }
			fallout_orientation_record_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Copies an authenticated installed package into the
# temporary request fields consumed by the character component. A two-candidate
# package writes an explicit empty third slot so stale temporary data cannot leak.
fallout_orientation_load_candidate_request = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_request_loaded = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	fallout_orientation_prepare_candidate_mapping = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_region = constant:fallout_region.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_region = constant:fallout_region.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_region = constant:fallout_region.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.none }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_is_usable = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_id = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_id }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_id = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_id }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_type = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_type }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_type = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_type }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_region = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_region }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_region = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_region }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_archetype = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_1_archetype }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_archetype = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_2_archetype }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_package_count = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.three } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_id = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_id }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_type = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_type }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_region = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_region }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_archetype = fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_slot_3_archetype }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_candidate_request_loaded = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		fallout_orientation_clear_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.package_not_current }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_candidate_mapping_is_current = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.payload_invalid }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_character_install_surface_is_approved = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_registry_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_diagnostic.install_surface_blocked }
		}
		fallout_orientation_record_candidate_registry_diagnostic = yes
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Freezes the strongest, weakest, and immediate-crisis
# resource identities from the committed current resource array. Exact ties keep
# the lowest stable resource id.
fallout_orientation_freeze_resource_extrema = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_primary_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.food }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_primary_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^1 }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_weakest_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.food }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_weakest_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^1 }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.food }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^1 }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = constant:fallout_survival_resource.clean_water
		end = constant:fallout_survival_resource.upper_bound
		value = fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index > fallout_orientation_frozen_primary_value } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_primary_resource = fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_primary_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index < fallout_orientation_frozen_weakest_value } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_weakest_resource = fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_weakest_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^fallout_orientation_resource_scan_index }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^2 < fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.clean_water }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^3 < fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.medicine }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 < fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.filters }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^7 }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Sums the current living population of this country
# by traversing the committed stable Fallout state array. No world iterator is
# opened, and later demographic change is observed from the live state value.
fallout_orientation_freeze_live_country_population = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_population_country_scope = THIS }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_country_population_total = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.fallout_survival_ledger_states
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = var:fallout_orientation_population_country_scope
				fallout_survival_state_identity_row_is_current = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_population_current = state_population_k }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_population_current = constant:fallout_runtime.population_scale }
			round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_state_population_current
			add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_country_population_total = fallout_orientation_state_population_current }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_country_population = fallout_orientation_country_population_total }
}

fallout_orientation_record_diagnostic = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic = fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic_generation = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic_day = global.num_days }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input fields use the fallout_orientation_requested_*
# names. The effect freezes the complete orientation payload before the pending
# marker is written and never changes scheduler activation state.
fallout_orientation_begin_component = {
	fallout_orientation_reconcile_stale_generation = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_orientation_transaction_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_component = fallout_orientation_transaction_component }
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_mode = fallout_orientation_transaction_mode }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_begin_request_is_valid = yes }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_schema_version = constant:fallout_orientation_schema.version }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = fallout_orientation_requested_component }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = fallout_orientation_requested_mode }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_status = constant:fallout_orientation_status.pending }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_region = fallout_region_id }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype = fallout_government_archetype }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_country_memory = fallout_country_memory_id }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_cause_memory = global.fallout_cause_memory_source }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_component = fallout_orientation_requested_component }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_cohesion = fallout_survival_cohesion_current }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_tailored_resource = fallout_orientation_requested_tailored_resource }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_food = fallout_survival_current_entries^1 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_clean_water = fallout_survival_current_entries^2 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_medicine = fallout_survival_current_entries^3 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_scrap = fallout_survival_current_entries^4 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_fuel = fallout_survival_current_entries^5 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_power = fallout_survival_current_entries^6 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_filters = fallout_survival_current_entries^7 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_shelter = fallout_survival_current_entries^8 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_recognition = fallout_survival_current_entries^9 }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_receiving_capacity = fallout_orientation_requested_receiving_capacity }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_receiving_shelter = fallout_orientation_requested_receiving_shelter }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_match = fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_match }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_regional_match = fallout_orientation_requested_regional_match }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_unresolved_crisis = fallout_orientation_requested_unresolved_crisis }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_kind }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value = fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_value }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_days = fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_days }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_token = fallout_orientation_requested_archetype_effect_token }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_count = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_id = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_id.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_region = constant:fallout_region.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_region = constant:fallout_region.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_region = constant:fallout_region.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_archetype = constant:fallout_government_archetype.none }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_count = fallout_orientation_candidate_package_count }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_id = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_id = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_id = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_id }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_type = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_type }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_type = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_type }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_type = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_type }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_region = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_region }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_region = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_region }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_region = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_region }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_archetype = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_1_archetype }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_archetype = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_2_archetype }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_archetype = fallout_orientation_requested_candidate_slot_3_archetype }
		}
		fallout_orientation_freeze_resource_extrema = yes
		fallout_orientation_freeze_live_country_population = yes
		var:fallout_successor_assignment_capital_state = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_state_target = THIS }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_state_grade = fallout_state_grade }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_state_phase = air_winter_phase }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_exposure = air_winter_exposure }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_shelter = air_winter_shelter_capacity }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_recovery = air_winter_recovery }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_adaptation = air_winter_adaptation }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_reclamation = air_winter_reclamation }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_supply = fallout_state_supply_access_current }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_target_population_current = state_population_k }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_target_population_current = constant:fallout_runtime.population_scale }
			round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_target_population_current
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_target_population = fallout_orientation_target_population_current }
			set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_frozen_source_population = fallout_orientation_target_population_current }
		}
		clear_variable = fallout_orientation_receiving_state_target
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = fallout_orientation_requested_receiving_state }
			event_target:fallout_orientation_requested_receiving_state = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_receiving_state_target = THIS }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_transaction_pending
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.input_invalid }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.registry_not_current }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_successor_assignment_country_row_is_current = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.allocation_not_current }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { fallout_survival_country_identity_row_is_current = yes }
					NOT = { fallout_survival_country_resource_row_is_durable = yes }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.survival_ledger_not_current }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = global.fallout_cause_memory_source }
					NOT = { check_variable = { global.fallout_cause_memory_source > constant:fallout_request_source.none } }
					check_variable = { global.fallout_cause_memory_source > constant:fallout_request_source.strategic_singularity }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.cause_memory_missing }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_regional_row_surface_is_approved = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.regional_row_missing }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_archetype_row_surface_is_approved = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.archetype_row_missing }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { fallout_orientation_memory_row_surface_is_approved = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.memory_row_missing }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					var:fallout_successor_assignment_capital_state = {
						exists = yes
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
						fallout_successor_assignment_capital_row_is_current = yes
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.state_target_missing }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation }
				NOT = { fallout_orientation_requested_national_has_affordable_branch = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.branch_unaffordable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis }
				NOT = { fallout_orientation_requested_resource_has_affordable_branch = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.branch_unaffordable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype }
				NOT = { fallout_orientation_requested_government_has_affordable_branch = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.branch_unaffordable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution }
				NOT = { fallout_orientation_candidate_registry_is_sufficient = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.candidate_registry_insufficient }
		}
		fallout_orientation_record_diagnostic = yes
	}
}

fallout_orientation_select_event_token = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.cleanup }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.closure } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.closure_human }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.closure_ai }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.national_human_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.national_ai_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.national_human_result } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.national_ai_result } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.capital_human_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.capital_ai_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.capital_human_result } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.capital_ai_result } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.resource_human_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.resource_ai_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.resource_human_result } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.resource_ai_result } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.government_human_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.hidden_ai } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.government_ai_root } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.human_visible } } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_event_token = constant:fallout_orientation_event_id.government_human_result } }

	}
}

fallout_orientation_issue_next = {
	fallout_orientation_reconcile_stale_generation = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_transaction_is_current = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = fallout_orientation_event_issued
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_component = fallout_orientation_transaction_component }
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_stage = fallout_orientation_transaction_stage }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root }
					check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.result }
					check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.cleanup }
					check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.closure }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.root } }
				set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.result } }
				set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days = fallout_orientation_due_day }
				subtract_from_variable = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days = global.num_days }
				if = { limit = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days < constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.cleanup } }
				set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.closure } }
				set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issue_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			}
			fallout_orientation_select_event_token = yes
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_component = fallout_orientation_transaction_component }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_mode = fallout_orientation_transaction_mode }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_stage = fallout_orientation_transaction_stage }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_branch = fallout_orientation_transaction_branch }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_event_token = fallout_orientation_selected_event_token }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_due_day = global.num_days }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.result } }
				set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_due_day = fallout_orientation_due_day }
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_issued_date = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
			fallout_orientation_dispatch_selected_event = yes
			set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_event_issued
		}
	}
}
