fallout_orientation_select_branch_candidate = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_id = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_type = constant:fallout_orientation_candidate_type.none }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.relief_administration } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_id = fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_id }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_type = fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_1_type }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.security_extraction } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_id = fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_id }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_type = fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_2_type }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.regional_institution } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_id = fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_id }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_selected_candidate_type = fallout_orientation_frozen_candidate_slot_3_type }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_choose_branch = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_orientation_root_event_is_authenticated = yes
			has_variable = fallout_orientation_requested_branch
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch < constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.upper_bound }
			fallout_orientation_branch_is_affordable = yes
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = fallout_orientation_requested_branch }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_branch = fallout_orientation_requested_branch }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.result }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_status = constant:fallout_orientation_status.result_scheduled }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_result_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_delay.national_orientation_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_result_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_delay.capital_condition_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_result_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_delay.immediate_resource_crisis_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_result_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_delay.government_archetype_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_result_delay_days = constant:fallout_orientation_delay.character_or_institution_days }
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_due_day = global.num_days }
		add_to_variable = { fallout_orientation_due_day = fallout_orientation_result_delay_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.household_workplace } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_household_food } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^2 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_household_water } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_household_filters } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.district_settlement } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_district_food } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_district_shelter } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_district_scrap } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.emergency_command } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_command_fuel } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_command_filters } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.national_command_scrap } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.seal_heat_civic } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_seal_filters } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_seal_fuel } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_seal_scrap } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.disperse_stores_offices } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_disperse_food } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_disperse_scrap } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^6 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_disperse_power } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.evacuate_poisoned_core } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_evacuation_fuel } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_evacuation_food } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.capital_evacuation_shelter } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.ration } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.resource_ration_cohesion } fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.requisition } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.resource_requisition_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.resource_requisition_cohesion } fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.resource_tailored_scrap } if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_tailored_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^6 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.resource_tailored_support } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_tailored_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.fuel } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.resource_tailored_support } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.consolidate } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.government_consolidate_food } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.government_consolidate_fuel } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.government_division_food } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.government_division_shelter } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.rival } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.government_rival_scrap } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.government_rival_fuel } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.relief_administration } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^3 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_relief_medicine } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_relief_food } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.security_extraction } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^5 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_security_fuel } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_security_scrap } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.regional_institution } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^6 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_institution_power } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_institution_shelter } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_cost.character_institution_scrap } }
			fallout_orientation_select_branch_candidate = yes
		}
		fallout_orientation_clear_issued_receipt = yes
		fallout_orientation_issue_next = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_component_can_choose = yes }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.branch_unaffordable }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_diagnostic_day = global.num_days }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_calculate_score = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.base }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_primary_value }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.household_workplace } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_food } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.district_settlement } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_shelter } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.emergency_command } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_fuel } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.seal_heat_civic } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_filters } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.disperse_stores_offices } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_food } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.evacuate_poisoned_core } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_fuel } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.consolidate } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_food } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.division } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_shelter } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_government_branch.rival } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_scrap } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.relief_administration } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_medicine } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.security_extraction } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_fuel } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_character_branch.regional_institution } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_power } }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary = fallout_orientation_score_primary_value }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_high compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_high_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_mid compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_mid_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_primary_value < constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_low } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_penalty = constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_low_penalty }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = fallout_orientation_score_penalty }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_match = constant:fallout_orientation_score.match_flag } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.archetype_match_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_regional_match = constant:fallout_orientation_score.match_flag } } add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.regional_match_bonus } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_recognition value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.recognition_high compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.recognition_high_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_recognition < constant:fallout_orientation_score.recognition_low } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_penalty = constant:fallout_orientation_score.recognition_low_penalty }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = fallout_orientation_score_penalty }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.clamp_minimum }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_target_exposure value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_band_1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_deduction_1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_target_exposure value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_band_2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_deduction_2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_target_exposure value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_band_3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_deduction_3 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_target_exposure value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_band_4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value = constant:fallout_orientation_score.exposure_deduction_4 } }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_support_score = fallout_orientation_frozen_target_recovery }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_support_score = fallout_orientation_frozen_target_adaptation }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = fallout_orientation_support_score min = constant:fallout_orientation_score.support_minimum max = constant:fallout_orientation_score.support_maximum }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_support_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.recovery_adaptation_factor }
	round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_support_score
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = fallout_orientation_support_score min = constant:fallout_orientation_score.support_minimum max = constant:fallout_orientation_score.support_score_maximum }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = fallout_orientation_support_score }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_unresolved_crisis > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } } subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_calculated_score = constant:fallout_orientation_score.unresolved_crisis_penalty } }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = fallout_orientation_calculated_score min = constant:fallout_orientation_score.clamp_minimum max = constant:fallout_orientation_score.clamp_maximum }
	round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_calculated_score
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_score = fallout_orientation_calculated_score }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_support = fallout_orientation_support_score }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction = fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction_value }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Input is a positive
# fallout_survival_cohesion_delta magnitude.
fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = {
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
	fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes
}

fallout_orientation_clamp_resources = {
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^1 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^2 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^3 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^4 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^5 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^6 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^7 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^8 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = fallout_survival_current_entries^9 min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
}

fallout_orientation_record_country_deaths = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_count = fallout_orientation_frozen_country_population }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_count = fallout_orientation_death_fraction }
	round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_death_count
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_request = fallout_orientation_death_count }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_remaining = fallout_orientation_death_request }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_applied_total = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_country_scope = THIS }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.fallout_survival_ledger_states
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_remaining > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
				is_owned_by = var:fallout_orientation_death_country_scope
				fallout_survival_state_identity_row_is_current = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_contract_supplied = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_requested = fallout_orientation_death_remaining }
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_minimum_remaining = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.minimum_remaining_people }
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_log_deaths = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_target_country = var:fallout_orientation_death_country_scope }
			set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_has_target_country = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
			apply_exact_state_civilian_population_loss = yes
			add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_applied_total = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_remaining = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_count = fallout_orientation_death_applied_total }
	fallout_orientation_record_exact_country_deaths = yes
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_requested = fallout_orientation_death_request }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_applied = fallout_orientation_death_applied_total }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
}

fallout_orientation_record_exact_country_deaths = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_count > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_change = fallout_orientation_death_count }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_is_civilian = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_apply_state_pop = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_record_state_ledger = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_target_country = THIS }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_has_target_country = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		chaos_meter_register_deaths = yes
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_requested = fallout_orientation_death_count }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_applied = fallout_orientation_death_count }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_current_result_deaths_applied = fallout_orientation_death_count }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_record_state_deaths = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_death_request = fallout_orientation_frozen_target_population }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_death_request = fallout_orientation_death_fraction }
	round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_state_death_request
	var:fallout_orientation_state_target = {
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_contract_supplied = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_requested = ROOT.fallout_orientation_state_death_request }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_minimum_remaining = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.minimum_remaining_people }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_log_deaths = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_target_country = OWNER }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_has_target_country = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		apply_exact_state_civilian_population_loss = yes
		set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_deaths_requested = ROOT.fallout_orientation_state_death_request }
		set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_deaths_applied = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
		set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_current_result_deaths_applied = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
}

fallout_orientation_apply_national_result = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.household_workplace } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } }
			add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_success_recognition }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_success_cohesion }
			fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } }
			add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_partial_recognition }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_partial_cohesion }
			fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
			subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_failure_recognition }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_failure_cohesion }
			fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = yes
			subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_household_failure_food }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_recognition < constant:fallout_orientation_deaths.national_authority_recognition_threshold } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_fraction = constant:fallout_orientation_deaths.national_authority } fallout_orientation_record_country_deaths = yes }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.district_settlement } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } } add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_success_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_success_cohesion } fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } } add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_partial_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_partial_cohesion } fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_failure_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_failure_cohesion } fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = yes subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_district_failure_shelter } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.emergency_command } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } }
			add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_command_success_recognition }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_command_success_cohesion }
			fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_modifier_days = constant:fallout_orientation_duration.command_authority_days }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = fallout_orientation_command_authority days = fallout_orientation_modifier_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } }
			add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_command_partial_recognition }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_command_partial_cohesion }
			fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_modifier_days = constant:fallout_orientation_duration.contested_command_days }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = fallout_orientation_contested_command days = fallout_orientation_modifier_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
			subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_command_failure_recognition }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.national_command_failure_cohesion }
			fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_modifier_days = constant:fallout_orientation_duration.command_resistance_days }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = fallout_orientation_command_resistance days = fallout_orientation_modifier_days }
		}
	}
	fallout_orientation_clamp_resources = yes
}

fallout_orientation_reset_state_deltas = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_recovery_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_adaptation_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_reclamation_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. The assigned capital is authenticated before any
# state field changes. Recovery is written to both the durable bonus and live
# value. Supply uses the sole clamp-owning state-ledger helper.
fallout_orientation_apply_authenticated_state_deltas = {
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_state_target_is_current = yes }
		var:fallout_orientation_state_target = {
			add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta }
			clamp_variable = { var = air_winter_exposure min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
			add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta }
			clamp_variable = { var = air_winter_shelter_capacity min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = air_winter_recovery_bonus } }
				set_variable = { air_winter_recovery_bonus = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			}
			add_to_variable = { air_winter_recovery_bonus = fallout_orientation_state_recovery_delta }
			add_to_variable = { air_winter_recovery = fallout_orientation_state_recovery_delta }
			clamp_variable = { var = air_winter_recovery_bonus min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = air_winter_recovery min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
			add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = fallout_orientation_state_adaptation_delta }
			clamp_variable = { var = air_winter_adaptation min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
			add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = fallout_orientation_state_reclamation_delta }
			clamp_variable = { var = air_winter_reclamation min = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.minimum max = constant:fallout_survival_numeric.maximum }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_state_supply_access_delta = fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta }
			fallout_state_apply_supply_access_delta = yes
		}
	}
}

fallout_orientation_apply_capital_result = {
	fallout_orientation_reset_state_deltas = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.seal_heat_civic } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_success_exposure }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_success_shelter }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_recovery_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_success_recovery }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_partial_exposure }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_partial_shelter }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_recovery_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_partial_recovery }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_failure_exposure }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_seal_failure_shelter }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
			var:fallout_orientation_state_target = {
				if = {
					limit = { infrastructure > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
					damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.building_damage }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_fraction = constant:fallout_orientation_deaths.capital_seal } fallout_orientation_record_state_deaths = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.disperse_stores_offices } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_success_shelter }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_adaptation_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_success_adaptation }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_success_supply }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_partial_shelter }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_adaptation_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_partial_adaptation }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_partial_supply }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_failure_shelter }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_shelter_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_disperse_failure_supply }
			multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_fraction = constant:fallout_orientation_deaths.capital_disperse } fallout_orientation_record_state_deaths = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.evacuate_poisoned_core } }
		fallout_orientation_apply_capital_evacuation = yes
	}
	fallout_orientation_apply_authenticated_state_deltas = yes
}

fallout_orientation_apply_capital_evacuation = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_intended = fallout_orientation_frozen_source_population }
	multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_intended = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_success_share }
	round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_migration_intended
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = fallout_orientation_migration_intended }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = fallout_orientation_frozen_source_population } multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_partial_share } round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_migration_movement }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = fallout_orientation_frozen_source_population }
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_failure_share }
		round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_migration_movement
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths = fallout_orientation_migration_intended }
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_failure_death_share }
		round_temp_variable = fallout_orientation_migration_deaths
	}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_total_request = fallout_orientation_migration_movement }
	add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_total_request = fallout_orientation_migration_deaths }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_requested = fallout_orientation_migration_total_request }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_total_request > fallout_orientation_frozen_receiving_capacity } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_total_request = fallout_orientation_frozen_receiving_capacity } set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_capacity_clamped }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = fallout_orientation_migration_total_request }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths > fallout_orientation_migration_total_request } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths = fallout_orientation_migration_total_request } }
	var:fallout_orientation_state_target = {
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_contract_supplied = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_requested = ROOT.fallout_orientation_migration_total_request }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_minimum_remaining = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.minimum_remaining_people }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.fallout_aftermath }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_log_deaths = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_target_country = OWNER }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_has_target_country = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		apply_exact_state_civilian_population_loss = yes
		set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_migration_applied = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_total_applied = fallout_orientation_migration_applied }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths > fallout_orientation_migration_total_applied } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths = fallout_orientation_migration_total_applied } }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = fallout_orientation_migration_total_applied }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement = fallout_orientation_migration_deaths }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_applied = fallout_orientation_migration_total_applied }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_moved = fallout_orientation_migration_movement }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths_applied = fallout_orientation_migration_deaths }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_movement > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } } var:fallout_orientation_receiving_state_target = { add_manpower = var:ROOT.fallout_orientation_migration_movement } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_migration_deaths > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_count = fallout_orientation_migration_deaths } fallout_orientation_record_exact_country_deaths = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_success_exposure }
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_partial_exposure }
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_runtime.negative_one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_exposure_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.capital_evacuation_failure_exposure }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.food } } add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 = fallout_orientation_crisis_delta } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.clean_water } } add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^2 = fallout_orientation_crisis_delta } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.medicine } } add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^3 = fallout_orientation_crisis_delta } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource = constant:fallout_survival_resource.filters } } add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 = fallout_orientation_crisis_delta } }
}

fallout_orientation_apply_resource_result = {
	fallout_orientation_reset_state_deltas = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.ration } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_crisis_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_ration_success_crisis } fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = yes add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_ration_success_recognition } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_crisis_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_ration_partial_crisis } fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = yes add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_ration_partial_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_ration_partial_cohesion } fallout_orientation_apply_cohesion_loss = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_ration_failure_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_modifier_days = constant:fallout_orientation_duration.ration_unrest_days } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = fallout_orientation_ration_unrest days = fallout_orientation_modifier_days } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.requisition } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_crisis_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_requisition_success_crisis } fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_crisis_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_requisition_partial_crisis } fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = yes subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_requisition_partial_recognition } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } } subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_requisition_failure_recognition } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_death_fraction = constant:fallout_orientation_deaths.resource_requisition } fallout_orientation_record_country_deaths = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_resource_branch.tailored } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_crisis_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_tailored_success_crisis } fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = yes set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_reclamation_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_tailored_success_reclamation } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_crisis_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_tailored_partial_crisis } fallout_orientation_apply_crisis_delta = yes set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_reclamation_delta = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_tailored_partial_reclamation } }
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
			subtract_from_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 = constant:fallout_orientation_result.resource_tailored_failure_scrap }
			var:fallout_orientation_state_target = {
				if = {
					limit = { infrastructure > constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
					damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.building_damage }
					set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_orientation_current_result_building_damage_applied = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	fallout_orientation_clamp_resources = yes
	fallout_orientation_apply_authenticated_state_deltas = yes
}

fallout_orientation_apply_archetype_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.recognition } }
		add_to_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.cohesion } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_survival_cohesion_delta = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
		fallout_survival_apply_cohesion_delta = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.recovery } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_recovery_delta = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.adaptation } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_adaptation_delta = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.reclamation } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_reclamation_delta = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.supply } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_state_supply_delta = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind = constant:fallout_orientation_archetype_effect_kind.stability_modifier } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_modifier_days = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_days }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = fallout_orientation_consolidated_authority days = fallout_orientation_modifier_days }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_finish_component = {
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_component = fallout_orientation_transaction_component }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_branch = fallout_orientation_transaction_branch }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_outcome = fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_score = fallout_orientation_score }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_score_primary = fallout_orientation_score_primary }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_score_support = fallout_orientation_score_support }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_score_exposure_deduction = fallout_orientation_score_exposure_deduction }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_status = constant:fallout_orientation_status.resolved }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_memory_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_memory_component = fallout_orientation_transaction_component }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_memory_branch = fallout_orientation_transaction_branch }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_memory_outcome = fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_identity_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_identity_region = fallout_orientation_frozen_region }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_identity_archetype = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_identity_country_memory = fallout_orientation_frozen_country_memory }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_identity_cause_memory = fallout_orientation_frozen_cause_memory }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_deaths_applied = fallout_orientation_current_result_deaths_applied }
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_building_damage_applied = fallout_orientation_current_result_building_damage_applied }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_crisis_resource = fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_resource }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_crisis_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_tailored_resource = fallout_orientation_frozen_tailored_resource }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_archetype_effect_kind = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_kind }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_archetype_effect_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_value }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_archetype_effect_token = fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype_effect_token }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition }
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_capital_branch.evacuate_poisoned_core }
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_migration_requested = fallout_orientation_migration_requested }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_migration_applied = fallout_orientation_migration_applied }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_migration_moved = fallout_orientation_migration_moved }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_last_migration_deaths = fallout_orientation_migration_deaths_applied }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = fallout_orientation_capacity_clamped }
			set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_last_migration_capacity_clamped
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = fallout_orientation_last_migration_capacity_clamped }
	}
	fallout_orientation_clear_transient_payload = yes
}

fallout_orientation_resolve_component = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_history_should_record = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = fallout_orientation_history_generation } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_history_should_record = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_history_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation } } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_history_should_record = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_history_component = fallout_orientation_transaction_component } } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_history_should_record = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_result_event_is_authenticated = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_orientation_result_applied } }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_current_result_deaths_applied = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { fallout_orientation_current_result_building_damage_applied = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
			fallout_orientation_calculate_score = yes
			if = { limit = { fallout_orientation_score_is_success = yes } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.success } }
			else_if = { limit = { fallout_orientation_score_is_partial = yes } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.partial } }
			else_if = { limit = { fallout_orientation_score_is_failure = yes } set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_outcome = constant:fallout_event_outcome_type.failure } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } } fallout_orientation_apply_national_result = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.capital_condition } } fallout_orientation_apply_capital_result = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis } } fallout_orientation_apply_resource_result = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype } } fallout_orientation_apply_government_result = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution } } fallout_orientation_apply_character_result = yes }
			set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_result_applied
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_orientation_component_to_record = fallout_orientation_transaction_component }
		fallout_event_record_orientation_component = yes
		if = {
			limit = { fallout_event_orientation_receipts_are_consistent = yes }
			fallout_orientation_record_history = yes
			fallout_orientation_finish_component = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = fallout_orientation_transaction_pending
			NOT = { fallout_orientation_state_target_is_current = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_diagnostic = constant:fallout_orientation_diagnostic.state_ledger_not_current }
		fallout_orientation_record_diagnostic = yes
	}
}

	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_event_token = fallout_orientation_issued_event_token }
	fallout_orientation_resolve_component = yes

fallout_orientation_begin_closure = {
	fallout_orientation_reconcile_stale_generation = yes
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_orientation_closure_is_eligible = yes
			has_variable = fallout_orientation_requested_mode
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_mode > constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.none }
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_mode < constant:fallout_event_dispatch_mode.upper_bound }
		}
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_schema_version = constant:fallout_orientation_schema.version }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.character_or_institution }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_mode = fallout_orientation_requested_mode }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.closure }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.none }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_status = constant:fallout_orientation_status.result_scheduled }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_region = fallout_orientation_identity_region }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_archetype = fallout_orientation_identity_archetype }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_country_memory = fallout_orientation_identity_country_memory }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_cause_memory = fallout_orientation_identity_cause_memory }
		set_country_flag = fallout_orientation_transaction_pending
		fallout_orientation_issue_next = yes
	}
}

fallout_orientation_schedule_cleanup = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			fallout_orientation_closure_event_can_enter = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_orientation_requested_event_token value = fallout_orientation_issued_event_token compare = equals }
		}
		fallout_orientation_clear_issued_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_stage = constant:fallout_orientation_stage.cleanup }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_status = constant:fallout_orientation_status.result_scheduled }
		fallout_orientation_issue_next = yes
	}
}

fallout_orientation_cleanup_event84 = {

	}
}

fallout_orientation_reconcile_stale_generation = {

	if = {

		fallout_orientation_clear_transient_payload = yes

		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_accepted = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_ai_apply_survival_weight = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.tertiary }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.branch_minimum } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.primary } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.two } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.secondary } }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_crisis_value }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.national_orientation } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.household_workplace } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_food }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_clean_water < fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_clean_water } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_filters < fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_filters } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.district_settlement } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_food }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_shelter < fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_shelter } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_scrap < fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_scrap } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.emergency_command } }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_fuel }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_filters < fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_filters } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_scrap < fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value = fallout_orientation_frozen_resource_scrap } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value < constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_low } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.strong_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_branch_need_value < constant:fallout_orientation_score.resource_mid } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.weak_factor }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.immediate_resource_crisis }
			fallout_orientation_resource_mapping_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_orientation_requested_branch value = fallout_orientation_resource_registry_ai_preferred_branch compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.cell_preference_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_component = constant:fallout_event_orientation_component.government_archetype }
			fallout_orientation_government_mapping_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { var = fallout_orientation_requested_branch value = fallout_orientation_government_registry_ai_preferred_branch compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight = constant:fallout_orientation_ai_weight.cell_preference_bonus }
	}
}

fallout_orientation_choose_ai_branch_and_schedule_result = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_score = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_event_token = fallout_orientation_issued_event_token }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.branch_minimum }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_branch_is_affordable = yes }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.branch_minimum }
		fallout_orientation_calculate_score = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score = fallout_orientation_score }
		fallout_orientation_ai_apply_survival_weight = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score = fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_score = fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.branch_minimum }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.two }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_branch_is_affordable = yes }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.two }
		fallout_orientation_calculate_score = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score = fallout_orientation_score }
		fallout_orientation_ai_apply_survival_weight = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score = fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight }
		if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score > fallout_orientation_ai_best_score } } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_score = fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.two } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one } }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.three }
	if = {
		limit = { fallout_orientation_branch_is_affordable = yes }
		set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.three }
		fallout_orientation_calculate_score = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score = fallout_orientation_score }
		fallout_orientation_ai_apply_survival_weight = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score = fallout_orientation_ai_need_weight }
		if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.zero } check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score > fallout_orientation_ai_best_score } } } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_score = fallout_orientation_ai_projected_score } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.three } set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one } }
	}
	set_variable = { fallout_orientation_transaction_branch = constant:fallout_orientation_national_branch.none }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_orientation_ai_best_found = constant:fallout_orientation_runtime.one } }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_orientation_requested_branch = fallout_orientation_ai_best_branch }
		fallout_orientation_choose_branch = yes
	}
}
